## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

This is a keynote stage deck with a strong, consistent visual identity: dark blue field, bold cyan headlines, one idea per slide, large illustrations and clear partner logo slides (5, 20, 29, 30). The route is easy to follow, from AI everywhere to Siliconomy, developer cloud, process roadmap and closing recap (64), and the roadmap timelines on 52 and 53 and the Moore's Law chart on 23 are clear. Weaknesses are consequential: small light captions on dark art on slides 12, 14, 34, 36 and 52 and the dense logo and member lists on 56 and 64 would be hard to read, and the chart on 23 has tiny axis labels. Many slides are decorative title cards with little standalone content, and the partner and guest pairs (4, 5, 6, 29, 30, 31) repeat, so the deck depends on the speaker.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent blue identity, the one-message headline slides such as 8, 10 and 13, and the clean timeline format on 52.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge the small captions and sub-labels on slides 12, 34, 36, 52 and 62 and raise their contrast against the busy art, and simplify the member list on 56.

**What would change the score:** I would cut repeated guest title and logo pairs and the duplicated build slides (such as 34 and 36, 53 and 52) to tighten the pace.

_Confidence medium. 66 of 66 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This deck is the keynote from Intel Innovation 2023, presented by CEO Pat Gelsinger. It opens with a forward-looking statements disclaimer, then introduces Gelsinger and the theme of bringing AI everywhere, from client and edge to network and cloud. A guest segment with ai.io, a sports science company, shows Intel hardware running a performance application, followed by a QR code for further information. The middle section covers the scale of the silicon industry, framed as a $574B silicon industry powering an $8 trillion global tech economy, and the idea of Siliconomy, with a growth figure for connected devices. It then moves to Intel Developer Cloud, Intel's process and foundry roadmap from Intel 7 through Intel 18A, a list of UCIe ecosystem partners, and a comparison of packaging technology. Later sections cover the Core Ultra and Xeon launches, quantum and neuromorphic research, and the Project Strata software initiative. The closing slides recap the roadmap, introduce a guest speaker, and present a notices page.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Intel Innovation 2023 keynote deck from Intel have?**

The deck has 66 slides. Slide 1 is the forward-looking statements disclaimer and the later slides close with a notices page.

**What does the Intel deck say about the size of the silicon industry?**

Slide 8 describes a $574B Silicon Industry powering an $8 Trillion Global Tech Economy. Slide 9 introduces the idea of Siliconomy as an evolving economy enabled by silicon.

**What growth figures does the Intel deck give for connected devices?**

Slide 10 states a 4x increase in connected devices over the past 5 years and a 15x growth in connected devices in 10 years. The source is a commissioned study by Forrester Consulting on behalf of Intel, December 2022.

**What process roadmap does the Intel deck show for its manufacturing nodes?**

Slide 52 lists Intel 7 as in high volume manufacturing now, Intel 4 as in high-volume ramp now, and Intel 3 as manufacturing ready and sampling in 2H 2023. It also shows Intel 20A on track for manufacturing in 2024 and Intel 18A on track, with silicon going into the fab in Q1 2024.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The bold headline over a dark, illustrated background sets the theme and the visual language used through the deck.

**Text**

Bringing AI Everywhere / from client and edge / to the network and cloud

**Layout**

Single text block left-aligned at about 18% from the left edge, sitting in the lower-middle of the slide, with the title above a two-line subtitle. Illustration fills the full canvas behind the text, with the cloud motif top-left and the laptop and code panels on the right. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed illustration covering the whole slide. Top-left: a large cyan outline cloud (about 20% to 31% of width, 0% to 17% of height) with a pixel-grid rain column falling from its base down the left third, the squares fading from light cyan #3FD3F5 at the top to deep blue #1D4FB8 at the bottom. Top-centre: a vertical stack of blue-to-cyan pixel-bar graphics, a bright white-cyan L-shaped neon line, and a small purple-and-white chip with three cyan traces descending from it. Centre-top: a rounded-corner outline panel (about 42% to 56% of width, 22% to 30% of height) holding a cyan cloud icon with a small cyan node on its right, a glowing cyan vertical bar beside it, and a blue code-line thumbnail. Right: a dark laptop (screen about 73% to 100% of width, 5% to 42% of height, keyboard base about 71% to 100%, 42% to 49%) with cyan, pink and violet code-line panels in three stacked windows on its right edge, connected by cyan bracket lines to a tall vertical teal-to-blue pixel column (about 77% to 86% of width, 34% to 82% of height). Circuit-trace lines run across the upper centre-right. Bottom-left: a small glowing blue-outlined cloud with two white chevrons, a green dotted line with a green ring, and a pink-orange-red code-line panel. Lighting is a self-lit neon glow; dominant colours are cyan #22C5F0, royal blue #1C5BD6, magenta-pink #E8436E and deep navy #00104D. Not cropped inside the slide except at the right edge where the code panels run off the canvas.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed illustration over a deep navy to royal blue gradient, from royal blue #0B4FC0 at top-left to midnight navy #000B45 at bottom. Deep navy-to-royal-blue background with a diagonal gradient from royal blue #0B4FC0 at top-left to midnight navy #000B45 at bottom. Overlaid on the athlete are thin white circular reticles with small mint-green (#3DE0B0) dot markers at the shoulder, elbow, hip, knee and hand, with the numeric readouts listed in the elements. Background behind the athlete carries concentric thin arcs and a tick-marked dial in teal and magenta. Ai.io logo in white with a green (#2ECC71) dot replacing the i-dot period. Name in semibold geometric sans (Intel Clear-like), cyan-blue #3CC8F5 with lighter gradient toward #8FE6FA at the right end. Subtitle lines in light geometric sans with wide letter spacing, regular weight, colour split: first words in white #FFFFFF and remaining words in light cyan #6FDDF7. Name cap height about 6.3% of slide height; subtitle about 3.6%. No shapes, borders or shadows on text. Photo has a dark navy overlay tint on the left and no rounded corners.

### Slide 5

**Text**

ai.io / + / intel / intel innovation / Balance / 89.9° / Distance / 14.6m / 30m Sprint / 4.18s / Reactions / 397ms / Fatigue / 88% / Agility / 760ms

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slide with the sprinter graphic filling the right two-thirds and the logo stack on the left. The ai.io logo, a plus sign and the intel logo are stacked on a central vertical axis at about x 14 to 35%. The intel innovation logo sits at bottom left, about 7% from the left edge and 8% from the bottom. No slide number or footer rule is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photographic and graphic background: a sprinter in a short-sleeved shirt and shorts, in mid-stride and leaning forward, with the right arm raised over the head and the left arm bent forward, rendered in grayscale with a blue-violet tint. The figure runs from the upper right toward the lower left. Translucent circular tracking rings sit on the shoulders, elbows, hip, knee and hand, each with a small glowing green-teal dot at its centre. Small white data callouts sit beside the figure: Balance 89.9° above the raised arm, Distance 14.6m beside the upper right hand, 30m Sprint 4.18s at mid-left, Reactions 397ms below the torso, Fatigue 88% beside the knee, and Agility 760ms (faint) at lower left. A vertical blue HUD bar graph with horizontal bars and a white highlight line runs down the far right edge. Concentric dark-blue and magenta-tinted arcs, a dashed tick-mark dial and a teal segmented ring surround the figure. Along the bottom right are neon line icons: a cyan cloud outline, a red and yellow double-chevron inside a blue rounded rectangle, green dotted circuit lines, and a vertical stack of red and orange horizontal bars. The composition is cropped at the top edge and right edge; the photo is full-bleed with a dark navy tint and overlay. Lighting is a cool, even key on the figure with blue rim glow. Dominant colours are navy #0A1450, violet-grey #8A8DBF and electric blue #1E6BFF. Logos: ai.io wordmark in white with a green dot #2BD97C replacing the dot of the i; a light-blue plus sign #3DC8F0; the Intel logo in white with a cyan square #00C7FD above the i; the intel innovation wordmark in white with the on in cyan #00C7FD.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy #0A1450 with a blue gradient glow at top left and the full-bleed athlete photograph overlaid. Dark navy background with a blue gradient glow at the top left fading to near-black navy at the bottom. Logos are white. The plus sign and the on in the footer are light cyan #3DC8F0. The green dot in ai.io is #2BD97C. Typefaces: ai.io and intel are geometric sans-serif logotypes in bold weight; intel innovation is a sans-serif logotype with a lighter weight for innovation. Data callout labels are small regular sans-serif in light grey #C8CCE6 with the numbers in white regular sans-serif. No rules or border lines. Shapes have no corner radius except the rounded icon frame. Glow and blur effects on the neon icons.

The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about x 8%, y 92%, and a neon circuit illustration fills the bottom-right corner; no page number or footer text is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a male sprinter in a grey-scale-tinted pose, mid-stride and leaning forward with arms extended, shown from the right side of the frame with the head at upper right (about x 75% to 88%, y 7% to 42%). Teal-green dot markers sit on the joints (shoulders, elbows, hip, knee, ankle) with thin white circular outlines around them. Overlaid white numeric readouts: Balance 89.9°, 30m Sprint 4.18S, Reactions 397MS, Distance 14.6M, Agility 760MS, Fatigue value partly obscured. Product captions: first line in light sky-blue #8FD8F5 regular weight, second line in bold cyan #35C8F2. Caption text about 2.2% of slide height; headline text about 4.4% of slide height; ai.io logo about 7.4% of height. ### Slide 7

**Text**

Balance / 89.9° / Distance / 14.6M / 30m Sprint / 4.18S / Reactions / 397MS / Fatigue / 88% / ai.io / Scan to learn more / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed photographic background with no title and no body text block. The ai.io logo sits left of centre at about 9.7% to 26.7% horizontally and 40% to 53% vertically. A square QR code sits centre-left at about 31% to 49% horizontally and 32% to 63% vertically, with the caption 'Scan to learn more' centred beneath it. The runner fills the right half. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% to 25% horizontally and 90% to 98% vertically. Margins are roughly 7% on the left. There is no page number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Background: a full-bleed monochrome photograph of a male sprinter in a short-sleeved shirt, mid-stride and leaning forward in a low start, moving from upper-right to lower-centre. The photo is desaturated to lavender-grey and blue-grey tones (about #B8BCE0 highlights, #3A3F78 shadows) with a navy tint over it. Thin glowing circles and green dots (#1ED760 approx.) mark joints on the arms, hip, knee and head. Labels float beside the markers: Balance 89.9° (top centre-right), Distance 14.6M (right, beside a vertical HUD of blue data bars), 30m Sprint 4.18S (left of the runner), Reactions 397MS (centre-right), Fatigue 88% (lower right). Glowing blue data bars run down the right edge. A teal-and-magenta concentric arc graphic curves around the runner. Along the bottom right, neon line icons appear: a blue chevron, a cloud outline in cyan, a red-yellow double-chevron badge in a blue outlined rectangle, a green dotted circuit line, and a vertical stack of orange and magenta bars. QR code: black-on-white square matrix (rendered as pale cyan #D6F4FF modules on navy) with standard three finder squares; placed as two identical 295 by 295 pixel images at the same position. ai.io logo: white bold geometric sans wordmark 'ai.io' with the dot of the 'i' in the second position replaced by a green (#1ED760) circle. intel innovation logo: white 'intel' wordmark in bold sans with a small registered mark, followed by 'innovation' in lighter weight, with the final 'on' in cyan (#00C7FD). Nothing is cropped; the photo is full-bleed; the QR and logos are unboxed.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy blue gradient (#0A3DA8 at top-left to #050B3A at bottom-right) with a desaturated monochrome photograph of a sprinter covering the full slide. Dark navy background with a deep blue gradient, brighter blue at the top-left (#0A3DA8) fading to near-black navy (#050B3A) at the bottom-right. Titles: none. Body text: white (#FFFFFF) for the ai.io wordmark and the data labels. Accent: green (#1ED760) for the logo dot and joint markers; cyan (#4FC3F7) for the caption and intel innovation's 'on'. Typefaces: the ai.io wordmark is a bold geometric sans-serif (close to Futura Bold). The caption is a light geometric sans-serif (close to Intel Clear Light) in mixed case, about 2.6% of slide height. Data labels are small sans-serif in mixed case, about 1.5% of slide height for the caption text labels and about 3% for their numeric values. Line and shape styles: thin 1 px translucent white circles around joints; blue and cyan neon outlines on the bottom icons with soft glow; no hard corners except the rectangle badge with small corner radii. Shadows: soft glow on the neon elements; gradients: background only.

### Slide 8

**Text**

$574B
Silicon Industry
powering
$8 Trillion
Global Tech Economy
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed photographic background with the text stacked in one centred column on the right, from the $574B figure at top to Global Tech Economy at bottom, with the red 'powering' tag between the two stat pairs. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left edge and 8% from the bottom. No title placeholder, footer, page number, or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph/illustration composite across the full slide. Left half: three overlapping silicon wafers seen as large discs, partly cropped by the slide edges. The upper-left wafer is blue-green with a grid of rectangular dies, the lower-centre wafer is magenta-pink and purple with a dense die grid and coloured speckles, and the lower-right wafer is amber, orange and red with vertical bands. Lighting is neon, with glowing cyan and magenta highlights. Centre-top: a vertical glowing cyan-white neon line forming an L-shaped circuit trace, a dotted cyan pixel-bar column, and a circular chip icon with a fingerprint icon in a rounded square at top right. Below it, a square-outlined processor chip with magenta-violet glow and a 3x3 grid of small dies with a white centre dot, and a small red LED chip with circuit traces. Bottom-right: a cyan outline cloud icon with two chevrons pointing left, a green dotted line, and a blue-outlined box with red triple chevrons. Bottom-left: thin cyan chevron. No charts, tables, or photographs of people. Overlay: dark navy gradient darkening the right side.

**Design**

Background is a dark navy-to-royal-blue gradient (#0A1A5C on the right, #1A4FD0 at top left), with the wafer photograph covering it. Stat numerals and labels use a light cyan to blue gradient (about #8EEFFF to #2FB6E8). The 'powering' label is white (#FFFFFF) on a solid red (#FF5662) rectangle with square corners and no shadow. Body text is light cyan (#5FD3F5). Typeface: geometric sans-serif. The large figures appear to be a medium/bold geometric sans (similar to IntelOneDisplay-Medium). The labels are a light, widely letter-spaced geometric sans (IntelOneDisplay-Light). Stat figure size is about 12.5% of slide height; labels about 4.6%. Glowing neon line shapes with soft blur, no rounded corners apart from the cloud and rounded square icons.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The paired Siliconomy stats are set large with a short caption, so one number carries each point.

**Text**

Siliconomy / noun: / An evolving economy enabled by the / magic of silicon where semiconductors / are essential to maintaining and / enabling modern economies. / intel innovation

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a large isometric illustration fills the left half, bleeding off the left and bottom edges, while the title and definition sit in a right-hand text column starting at about 55% of slide width. The title is at upper right, with 'noun:' and the four-line definition below it, left-aligned with a hanging indent. The intel innovation logo sits at bottom left, and decorative circuit panels run along the bottom right. There is no visible footer, page number or date.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark navy circuit-board background illustration. Main visual is an isometric 3D city of glowing towers and blocks on a circular silicon wafer, with a grid pattern on the wafer surface. Towers are blue, violet, magenta and pink with white window stripes; a cylindrical tower sits in the foreground. The wafer is lit in violet, teal and blue, and its rim is pale green-teal. Smaller stylised elements include a cloud icon in a rounded outlined panel at top left, a microchip with glowing dots at top, a glowing white circuit trace at top, a fingerprint icon in a panel and a document-with-checkmark icon in a panel at lower right, a bar-chart panel with checkbox list, a grid of blue and magenta squares at bottom, a circular icon near the bottom centre, a red and yellow double-chevron button at bottom right and a blue chevron at bottom centre-right. The Siliconomy title and definition text are rendered as raster images.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy blue (#0A1A4A) gradient with circuit illustration, fading to deep purple at the left edge (#3A0F4A).

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 10

**Text**

Siliconomy / 4x / Increase / in connected devices / over past 5 years / 15x / Growth / in connected devices / in 10 years / Base: 641 security decision-makers at consumer electronic device organizations / Source: A commissioned study conducted by Forrester Consulting on behalf of Intel, December 2022 / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slide with a large isometric city illustration occupying the left half, from roughly x 0 to 52 percent and y 22 to 90 percent. The right half holds a centred stack of text: the title Siliconomy at the top, then two stat blocks side by side (4x at left, 15x at right) with their labels beneath, and a small two-line source note below them. The Intel innovation logo sits at bottom left, about x 7 y 90. Circuit-board and dashboard illustrations run around the edges and along the bottom right. There is no visible slide number or footer rule.

**Visuals**

Isometric illustration of a city built on a circular platform. The platform is a translucent lilac-to-teal disc with a fine grid of lines. Tall glowing skyscrapers in blue, magenta and pink rise from it, with lit window stripes and rooftop cubes. A cylindrical storage tower sits at lower centre-left and a small glowing cube sits near the platform edge. Above the city are circuit-board motifs: a cloud icon in a rounded rectangle at upper left, a microchip with pins at top centre, a glowing pixel grid at top left, and traces with dots running to the right. Along the lower right, dashboard panels show a fingerprint icon, a document with a checkmark, a bar chart with check-list rows, a keyboard-like grid of blue and magenta bars, and a red chevron button. The palette is deep blue with cyan, magenta and violet glows; the lighting is neon and the framing is wide. No chart axes or data series are present beyond the decorative bars.

**Design**

Background: Deep navy blue gradient, about #0A1A5C at the top right shading to #001A6E, with a violet-magenta glow at the left edge near #5A1F6E.

### Slide 11

**Text**

intel® / Developer Cloud / cloud.intel.com / access to our latest / accelerated hardware and software / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed background illustration with the text block placed in the right half, left-aligned, inside a large cloud outline; the isometric city platform fills the left half. The title sits at about 40% of slide height, the cloud outline spans the right side, and the intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% margin. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed digital illustration: an isometric city of glowing blue, violet and magenta office towers and cylinders rising from a circular, grid-lined platform in the left half, with a glowing cyan-to-violet gradient base. Surrounding circuit-board motifs in cyan and magenta line art: a cloud icon in a rounded box at upper left, a fingerprint and document cards in the lower centre, a dashboard card with bar chart and checklist lower centre, a chevron arrow icon and a red and yellow double-chevron icon lower right, and dotted connector lines. Behind the text, a large outlined cloud shape with a gradient stroke running from cyan on the left through blue to red-pink at the top right, about 4 px wide with a soft glow. Lighting is neon-style, dark-background, emissive. Dominant colours: navy #001A6E, electric blue #1E5BFF, cyan #22D3EE, magenta #E0307A. Not cropped; the illustration covers the whole slide.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy blue gradient #001A6E to #0A2A8C with a purple-magenta haze at the lower left, behind a full-bleed neon circuit illustration. Dark navy blue gradient background, darker to the right and lighter blue at upper left with a purple-magenta tint at lower left. Title in white #FFFFFF, geometric sans-serif (Intel Clear-like), regular weight; 'intel' wordmark in bold white. URL in bold light cyan #5CD8F2. Body text in light cyan #7FE3F5, light-weight geometric sans-serif. Title about 6.5% of slide height, body about 2.6%. Cloud outline is a 4 px gradient stroke with soft glow and no fill. Circuit lines are thin cyan #22D3EE strokes; boxes have 45-degree cut corners and thin blue #2F6BFF outlines. Shadows and glows are soft.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: small light captions on dark art on slides 12, 14, 34, 36 and 52 and the dense logo and member lists on 56 and 64 would be hard to read, and the chart on 23 has tiny axis labels.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small captions and sub-labels on slides 12, 34, 36, 52 and 62 and raise their contrast against the busy art, and simplify the member list on 56.

**Text**

Build and test for / best performance / efficiency
Advanced AI / capabilities / toolkits
Multiplatform / systems and / clusters
intel Developer Cloud
cloud.intel.com
Available to all
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark navy background with a left column of three stacked full-width panels (x 3.6 to 47.7, y 13.1 to 87.4), each holding a text label at its left and its visuals at its right. The right half carries the intel Developer Cloud lockup inside a large gradient-outlined cloud, with the tagline Available to all beneath the URL. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left below the panels. There is no title placeholder, no slide number and no footer text.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration of dark navy circuit-board graphics with glowing cyan and magenta chip and trace patterns across the top and bottom edges. Row 1, Build and test for best performance efficiency: five 3D product renders in a row on a light blue gradient panel, each a dark chip package with a white intel logo: Intel XEON (x 18.5), Intel XEON MAX SERIES (x 23.4), Intel DATA CENTER GPU (x 28.3), Intel DATA CENTER GPU MAX SERIES (x 33.4), Intel GAUDI (x 39.1); all at y about 17 to 34. Row 2, Advanced AI capabilities toolkits: oneAPI logo (white stylised numeral 1 with a cyan square and the word oneAPI in white) and OpenVINO logo (white wordmark with two stylised eye-like o letters, trademark mark at upper right). Row 3, Multiplatform systems and clusters: three white line icons on the blue panel: a hand-with-pen and grid icon, a cloud-with-bar-chart icon, and a gear icon. Behind the cloud, at lower right of the panel area, is a dark navy illustration of UI cards with a fingerprint icon, a checklist card, a bar and line chart card and a grid of blue and magenta bars, all outlined in glowing cyan-blue strokes. Small dotted cyan connector line runs from the left panel edge to the cloud at y about 63.8. Bottom-right, a magenta and yellow chevron arrow tag. Cloud is a thick outlined shape with a gradient stroke from cyan on its left to coral red on its upper right, with a navy fill.

**Design**

Background is a deep navy gradient from #001A5C at lower right to #0A2A7A at upper left with cyan #00C8F0 and magenta #E8478B circuit accents. Panels use a gradient from medium blue #0A5FA8 at left to bright cyan-blue #1AAEE0 at right, with no border. Panel label text is white #FFFFFF for the first panel and light blue #8FDCFA for the second and third. Accent colour for cloud.intel.com is cyan #3FD0F5. Tagline Available to all is coral red #FF5662. The wordmark and Developer Cloud text are white #FFFFFF. Typefaces: Intel One Display, a geometric sans-serif, medium weight for panel labels and light weight for the tagline; the wordmark and Developer Cloud in a regular-weight sans-serif. Panel label size is about 2.6 percent of slide height; tagline is 5.93 percent. Panels have square corners, no shadows and no outlines. Cloud outline stroke is about 1 percent of slide height thick with a soft glow.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The Developer Cloud offer is reduced to a single large message on a clean background for emphasis.

**Text**

cloud.intel.com
Intel-optimized
AI Software
supported by the latest
CPUs, NPUs and GPUs
1 week free
of Intel Developer Cloud access
for every in-person attendee
it
Starts
Here
import webbrowser as siliconomy
accessibleDevelopment = “https://cloud.intel.com”
devNeeds = {"Open": True, "Choice": True,
"Trust": True, "AI": True,
"Client": True, "Edge": True,
"Cloud": True }
if any(x is True for x in devNeeds.values()):
siliconomy.open(accessibleDevelopment)

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a wide 16:9 slide. The left text stack (URL, two-line headline, subtitle, offer lines) is left-aligned starting at about 19% of slide width and sits at mid-height, roughly 40% to 84% of slide height. The right code panel is a thin white bracket frame from about 47% to 94% of width and 36.6% to 79% of height, with a red 'it Starts Here' tile sitting on its top-left corner; the frame is open at the top-right. There is no title placeholder, footer, page number or date.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a large glowing cloud silhouette formed by a thick luminous ring stroke, running from the lower-left edge up over the top and down the right side, with its bottom sweeping across the lower part of the slide. The ring is a gradient from bright cyan #00C8FF through violet #8A4FD0 to coral red #FF5662, with soft outer glow. Top-right: a cyan outline cloud icon (about 88% to 97% x, 3% to 9% y) with a glowing blue chevron to its left, a small octagonal tech tile with orange chevrons '>>>' (about 75% to 82% x, 9% to 16% y), thin green dotted lines and a green glowing ring dot to the right; a faint blue circuit outline frames the top-right. Left edge (about 0% to 13% x, 63% to 100% y): a stylised code-minimap graphic of stacked horizontal bars in orange, red, pink, magenta and cyan on dark navy, with a glowing vertical blue bar at about 2% x and a glowing yellow vertical bar near 4% x, lit by neon glow. Dominant colours are electric blue, cyan, coral red and violet. Full-bleed, no cropping or boxing, no tint overlay beyond the glow. Code panel is monospace text with no syntax image.

**Design**

Background is a deep navy field with a cobalt glow at top-left, #0050C8 fading to #000A3A at bottom-right. Headline 'Intel-optimized AI Software' is light cyan #5CCBFF to #29B6F6 in a geometric sans (IntelOne Display Medium), size about 6.2% of slide height, cap height about 4.8%. 'cloud.intel.com' is cyan #5CCBFF, medium weight, about 3.0%. Subtitle lines are white #FFFFFF in IntelOne Display Light, about 3.6% size. 'it Starts Here' tile text is white light sans with wide letter-spacing. 'Week free' offer is red #FF5662 medium at 2.91%, the two following lines white light at 2.50%. Code is white #FFFFFF monospace with keywords in red #FF5662, strings and True in cyan #00C8FF, and the module name siliconomy in olive green #9BC53D; size about 2.6%. Line work: white 2px-equivalent stroke bracket with square corners. Red tile #FF5662 with sharp corners, no shadow. White small square #FFFFFF at the tile's top-right corner. Gradients and glows on the cloud ring and left graphic; no drop shadows on text.

- illustration at x 52, y 2, w 46, h 18 [#00C8FF]: Top-right tech icons: cyan cloud outline icon, blue chevron, octagon tile with orange chevrons, green dotted lines and dot (Neon outline style with glow; cloud icon about 88-97% x, 3-9% y; octagon tile about 75-82% x, 9-16% y; green dotted lines #22DD66 about 1px dots)

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: small light captions on dark art on slides 12, 14, 34, 36 and 52 and the dense logo and member lists on 56 and 64 would be hard to read, and the chart on 23 has tiny axis labels.

**Text**

intel® ignite / FUEL YOUR FIRE / Artificial Intelligence / Next Generation Systems / Platforms and Edge to Cloud / intel innovation

**Layout**

Three equal tiles sit in a horizontal row across the lower-middle of the slide, each with a caption below it. The intel ignite logo sits top-left, the FUEL YOUR FIRE wordmark with flame runs across the upper right of the logo, and the intel innovation logo sits bottom-left. There is no title placeholder, no footer text and no slide number; margins are roughly 7% left and 4% top.

**Visuals**

Three square icon tiles, each a blue gradient box with a white and cyan line icon: (1) Artificial Intelligence: a white hexagon-like bracket on the left, a cyan circuit-patterned human brain profile on the right, with cyan dashed connector lines. (2) Next Generation Systems: a cyan and white circuit-line pattern ending in a white box with a right-pointing arrow, and a white gear at the bottom. (3) Platforms and Edge to Cloud: a cyan cloud outline at top with a circuit line running down into a white and cyan hexagonal node network of connected circles. Also a cyan flame outline (thin line, white/cyan stroke) to the left of the FUEL YOUR FIRE wordmark, and the intel innovation logo. Behind each tile is a dark navy translucent panel (#000D4E at 70% opacity) that extends below the tile to hold the caption.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photographic-style blue abstract light-wave illustration over dark navy (#000D4E to #00103A) with cyan, magenta and orange light streaks. Dark navy to royal blue background with glowing blue light streaks at the top and a magenta, orange and cyan light ribbon sweeping across the lower half. The intel ignite logo is white 'intel' with a cyan 'ignite'. FUEL YOUR FIRE is an outlined, unfilled white thin-stroke display wordmark in uppercase. Captions are cyan #00C7FD in IntelOneDisplay Light, sentence case, about 2.7% of slide height, left-aligned, with tight line spacing. Tiles are sharp-cornered squares with a blue-to-cyan gradient fill; there are no shadows on tiles.

### Slide 15

**Text**

intel
ignite
DEEP RENDER
Solving too much data
and too little bandwidth problem
using Intel Developer Cloud
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue background image. Stacked logo column at the left margin (about 8% from the left edge): Intel Ignite wordmark at top, square AI icon below it, then the DEEP RENDER label, then a three-line title block indented to about 19% from the left, with the Intel Innovation footer logo at bottom left. No slide number, no footer text beyond the logo. The right half is open for the particle face image.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style digital illustration, full-bleed: a head-and-face silhouette built from small glowing cubes/dots in cyan-blue (about #2AA8F0 to #1A6FD8), occupying the right side from about x 60 to x 90 and y 5 to y 80, with a dispersal of scattered dots and fine diagonal light streaks drifting up-left. Behind it, a sweeping wave of translucent ribbons in orange, magenta, pink and violet rising from the bottom right corner to the right edge. Lighting is a glow from the cubes against a deep navy field. Composition is full-bleed with no border or crop. Logos: Intel Ignite wordmark (white 'intel' with cyan 'ignite', about 8.4% x, 8.6% y, 9.3% w, 12.6% h); square icon with a cyan hexagon outline on the left joined to a cyan brain outline on the right, on a blue gradient tile (about 8.3% x, 24.8% y, 10.4% w, 17.7% h); DEEP RENDER wordmark in black pixel-style capitals on a pale lavender-white box (about 18.7% x, 42.4% y, 27.0% w, 9.7% h); Intel Innovation logo in white (about 7.1% x, 90.2% y, 18.2% w, 7.7% h).

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient from bright cobalt #0A5FD0 at top to navy #050F4A at bottom, with a purple-navy band at bottom right. Title lines are cyan #29C8F2 bold geometric sans-serif (Intel Clear style), line one and two in bold weight, line three in regular light weight in a lighter cyan #5FD8F5 with wide letter-spacing. Body text is the same cyan family. Accent colours are cyan #1EC8F0, white #FFFFFF and orange-magenta in the wave. Title text height is about 3.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.8%. No shadows, no rounded corners; the logo tiles are square and sharp-edged.

### Slide 16

**Text**

intel GAUDI / Commitment to address / every phase of the AI / continuum / Performance metrics based on MLPerf Inference v3.1 benchmark. For configuration details, / see the results published by MLCommons. Results may vary. / intel innovation

**Layout**

Left-aligned text column starts at about 8% of slide width with the intel GAUDI logo at top, then the three-line headline, then a small disclaimer, with the intel innovation logo at bottom left near 91% height. The Gaudi 2 accelerator board is placed on the right, angled diagonally, bleeding toward the right edge, with vertical code-stream panels along the far right edge. No title placeholder, page number or footer text besides the logo lockup.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration of a dark blue digital scene. Central photograph of an Intel Gaudi 2 HL-series accelerator card, a blue printed circuit board with a large silver heatsink-covered processor labelled GAUDI 2 in grey, surrounded by capacitors, connectors and mounting holes, tilted roughly 30 degrees, lit from above with bright highlights on the metal heatsink and cool blue light on the board. The board overlaps the right half of the slide and is not cropped. Along the right edge are four vertical stacks of glowing code-like bars in cyan, magenta, red and pink on dark blue, connected by thin cyan circuit traces. Behind the board's left edge, horizontal blue and violet light streaks fade out toward the left. Along the bottom, thin glowing blue and green dotted circuit lines connect a red glowing chevron icon inside a blue-outlined hexagon, a cyan-outlined cloud icon with a blue chevron arrow, and a blue chevron arrow at bottom left. No charts, tables or photographs of people.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient, lighter cobalt #0059B8 at top left fading to near navy #001257 at bottom right. Headline is set in a geometric sans-serif (close to Intel One Display) in medium weight with a left-to-right gradient from bright cyan #22CDF5 to light blue #3FA6EC. Body disclaimer text is white #FFFFFF in regular-weight geometric sans, very small at about 1.1 percent of slide height, with the hyperlink in cyan #00C7FD and underlined. Headline cap height is about 4 percent of slide height. Logos are white #FFFFFF with cyan #00C7FD accent on the intel innovation o. Accent lines are glowing blue #1E7BFF and green #22E06A. No boxes, borders or corner radii; glow effects on icons and lines.

### Slide 17

**Text**

intel GAUDI / stability.ai / Large AI Supercomputer / will be a top 15 in the world / intel innovation

**Layout**

Left-aligned text stack in the upper-left third, starting at about 8% from the left edge: logo lockup, then the two-line headline, with the Intel Innovation logo at bottom left near 7% from the bottom. The Gaudi 2 board photo fills the right half, angled and bleeding off the right edge. There is no title placeholder, footer text, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a Habana Gaudi 2 AI accelerator board: a blue printed circuit board tilted about 30 degrees, with a large square silicon package labelled GAUDI2 in the centre and heat-sink-like pins along two edges, lit from above with white highlights on the chip and cool blue shadows. The board sits over a dark navy background and is not boxed. Decorative elements: horizontal glowing blue and violet speed lines fanning out to the left of the board; vertical columns of code-like bars in cyan, blue, pink, magenta and red on the far right edge, connected by thin cyan circuit traces; a small blue grid pattern of squares above the board's right edge; a glowing cyan outline cloud and a glowing blue chevron arrow at the bottom centre; a red-orange chevron-arrow icon inside a blue outlined tag shape at bottom centre with green dotted lines running left and right to glowing green and blue nodes; a blue chevron pointing left at bottom left beside the Intel Innovation logo. Logos: intel GAUDI lockup (white), stability.ai wordmark (white with a red full stop after 'stability'), intel innovation lockup (white with 'on' in cyan).

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed deep blue gradient from cobalt #0A4FB0 (top left) through navy #001A5C to dark navy #00103A (bottom right), with the Gaudi board photo and decorative graphics layered over it. Full-bleed deep blue gradient background, lighter cobalt #0A4FB0 at top left fading to navy #001A5C and near-black navy #00103A toward the lower right. Shapes are thin glowing neon lines with soft blue glow; no shadows on text; the board has a slight drop shadow.

### Slide 18

**Text**

“Dell Technologies and Intel are collaborating to offer AI / solutions to meet customers wherever they are on their / AI journey. Combining the reliability of Dell PowerEdge / servers – the industry’s top selling portfolio – with Intel / technologies for general purpose and accelerated / compute provides powerful systems for optimized / AI. PowerEdge systems with Xeon and Gaudi will / support AI workloads ranging from large scale / training to base level inferencing. We look forward to / helping customers transform their business with new / applications with this powerful combination.”
Jeff Clarke
Vice Chairman and COO, Dell Technologies
intel innovation

**Layout**

Single-column quote layout on the left half: a large quote block in the upper-left with the attribution beneath it, and a vertical stack of the Dell logo, a plus sign and the Intel logo on the right third. Text is left-aligned at about 8.7% from the left edge; there is no title or slide number. The Intel Innovation wordmark sits in the bottom-left corner at about 8% from the left and 92% from the top, and a neon circuit footer runs along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration: a dark blue digital scene with glowing neon circuit lines, a cloud icon and chevrons at the bottom, and on the right edge a vertical stack of server-like data panels (cyan, magenta, pink and blue horizontal bars with small square grids and connector lines). Bottom centre: a neon outlined box with three red-orange chevrons pointing right, linked by green dotted lines to a green glowing node on the right. Bottom right of centre: a glowing blue outlined cloud with a blue chevron to its left. A large light-blue quotation mark graphic at top-left of the text, with a soft gradient from #2A8FE8 to #1B5FC4. Dell logo: white ring with the word DELL in white bold capitals inside, on transparent background. Plus sign: thin cyan #4FD8F5 cross with rounded ends. Intel logo: white bold lowercase wordmark 'intel' with a small blue square accent over the i. Matching data-grid square pattern in blue and teal sits in a column behind the server panels.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient running from bright blue #0A5FD0 at the top to navy #001048 at the bottom-right. Quote body text is light sky blue #9FDDF8 in a light geometric sans-serif (Intel Clear Light style), regular weight, roughly 3.5% of slide height, with generous line spacing (about 4.7% per line). Highlighted phrases are bold with a coral-to-pink gradient fill from #FF8FA0 to #FFB7A8. Attribution name 'Jeff Clarke' is bold, in pale cyan #7FE3FF, about 3.6% of slide height. Title line 'Vice Chairman and COO, Dell Technologies' is light sky blue #7FD6F0, regular weight, about 2.6% of slide height. Logos are white #FFFFFF. Quote mark graphic is pale blue #3E9BE8. Neon accents are cyan #22E5F5, magenta #E04BD6 and red-orange #FF4B3E with a soft outer glow. No shadows on text; no rounded boxes.

### Slide 19

**Text**

4th Gen / Intel® Xeon® / Scalable Processor / MLPerf AI inference performance / results: addressing every phase / of the AI continuum / Performance metrics based on MLPerf Inference v3.1 benchmark. For configuration details, / see the results published by MLCommons. Results may vary. / intel innovation

**Layout**

Left-aligned text block sits in the left half, starting at about 15% of slide width. The title stack occupies roughly 33% to 62% of slide height, the subtitle sits beneath it, and a small footnote sits at about 69% to 71%. The Xeon box logo is in the top-left corner at about 4% from the top, and the Intel innovation logo is bottom-left at about 90% from the top. The right half is a full-height illustration with the boxed chip photo rising across the centre-right. There is no page number or footer band.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed digital illustration in blue. A cyan outlined cloud sits at the top centre-right, with a column of blue pixel squares falling beneath it. A second column of cyan and blue pixels sits to its right. Four stacked code-editor-style panels run down the right edge in cyan/blue, pink/purple, red/pink and magenta/blue, each with horizontal bars and small square markers, joined to the chip by thin cyan connector lines. A translucent purple-outlined chip with a glowing white dot sits below the photo, with circuit traces running to the bottom-right. The product photograph is a silver 4th Gen Intel Xeon Scalable Processor package, tilted, with the Intel XEON wordmark and the caption 4th Gen Intel Xeon Scalable Processor printed on its lid. It is lit from the upper left, is not cropped, and has a soft shadow below. A small Xeon box logo (blue cube with intel and XEON) sits at top left. Logos: Intel Xeon box logo, Intel XEON wordmark on chip, Intel innovation logo at bottom left.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient from bright azure #0A6FE0 at top left to midnight navy #000A3A at bottom right. Title lines use a horizontal gradient from #1E9BFF to #00C7FD to #0C8FB8 in a geometric sans (Intel One Display Medium look), weight medium, about 6.5% of slide height, tight leading. Subtitle is light-weight sans (Intel One Display Light), wide letter-spacing, cyan-blue #3FC8F0 to #9FE3F7, about 2.7% of slide height, leading about 3.2%. Footnote is regular sans, white #FFFFFF, about 1.1% of slide height; the link is cyan #00C7FD underlined. Lines are thin cyan #00C7FD strokes about 0.3% of slide width. The cloud outline is a 4 px cyan #00C7FD stroke with rounded lobes. Panels have square-cornered bars. No hard shadows apart from the chip's soft drop shadow.

### Slide 20

**Text**

Alibaba.com
+
intel
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed wide-screen composition with the logo lockup stacked in the left-centre third (Alibaba.com, plus sign, intel wordmark) and the intel innovation logo at bottom left. The right half is occupied by the illustration. No title, page number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed digital illustration on the right half and background. A cyan outlined cloud at top centre-right, with a column of blue and cyan square pixels falling from its base like rain. Beneath it, a teal-to-blue pixel column rises into a stack of four server-rack panels on the right, each drawn as horizontal bars of coloured code lines (cyan, pink-red, magenta-purple, and purple-blue) connected by thin cyan circuit lines. At centre-right, a glowing square processor chip with a grid of small lit squares, a white dot in the centre and purple-magenta glow, with blue traces running down from it. At bottom right, circuit traces run diagonally to a small glowing red-and-blue chip. Lighting is self-lit neon on a dark background. Dominant colours are electric blue, cyan, magenta and violet. Nothing is cropped except the cloud and racks at the top and right edges.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient, brighter cobalt blue #0A6FE0 at top left fading to navy #001050 at the bottom and centre. The Alibaba and intel logos are white #FFFFFF. The plus sign is light cyan #3FD3F5. The illustration uses cyan #19D4F2 and magenta #C2228F accents. No typeset text, so no typefaces apply beyond the logo wordmarks. Logo wordmarks are sans-serif. No borders, shadows or corner radii on the slide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue slide with the title block left-aligned at about 24.5% from the left and 18% from the top, above a horizontal five-step metric row across the centre. Two chip photos sit on a timeline arrow at the bottom centre, the Gaudi 2 chip at left and the Gaudi 3 chip at right, with their labels beneath. Decorative circuit and code-bar illustrations fill the left edge, the Intel innovation logo sits bottom-left, and there is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-slide background photograph/illustration of a dark blue digital scene with glowing blue pixel grid at top-left, a purple-cyan chip illustration at about x 10.5% y 27.5% (w 12%, h 20%) with blue circuit traces running down to a small red-and-blue glowing chip at about x 12% y 52% and further to the lower-left; a vertical bar of cyan and white light at about x 4% y 68%; a stacked red, orange and cyan horizontal bar chart-like code illustration along the bottom-left from about x 7% y 69% to x 25% y 100%, with a blue pixel grid at bottom-left corner. Photo 1: Intel Gaudi 2 chip, square-cornered rectangular package photo at x 25.3% y 43.4% w 12% h 19.6%, dark black package with gold/orange heat-spreader corners and a central dark die labelled GAUDI2, boxed with no border, sharp corners. Photo 2: Intel Gaudi 3 chip, portrait rectangular package photo at x 78.9% y 41.5% w 10.5% h 23%, black package with two dark dies and pinkish-purple and blue memory stacks on the left and right edges, labelled GAUDI3 on the top die, sharp corners. Metric numbers and labels are white and cyan text with no icons. Timeline: a thin light cyan (#7FD8F0) horizontal arrow line at x 17.4% y 68% w 78.1% h 1.7%, arrowhead at the right end; small white tick markers at x 29.5% and x 82.7% pointing up to the two chips. Between the metrics are dotted connector lines: cyan dots from the Gaudi 2 chip to 4x, blue dots between 4x and 2x, lilac-blue dots between 2x and 1.5x, pink dots between 1.5x Network and 1.5x HBM, and red-coral dots from the last 1.5x to the Gaudi 3 chip; these form one dotted line at x 37.1% y 53.9% w 41.6% h 0.7%. Intel innovation logo: white 'intel' wordmark with 'innovation' in white and the 'on' in cyan, at x 7.1% y 90.2% w 18.2% h 7.7%.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient from a brighter cobalt blue #0B3F9E at top-centre to a near-black navy #00093A at bottom. Title 'AI Roadmap' is bold geometric sans-serif (Intel Clear Bold style) in a horizontal gradient from light aqua #5FE3F5 at left to cobalt #1E8FD9 at right, about 9.3% of slide height em. Subtitle 'Bringing AI Everywhere' is regular-weight geometric sans-serif with wide letter-spacing in a cyan-to-teal gradient #7FE8F7 to #2CC3E0, about 6% em. Metric numerals (4x, 2x, 1.5x, 1.5x) are bold sans-serif in a cyan-to-blue gradient #5BE0F5 to #2FA8E8, about 5.6% em; metric labels are regular sans-serif in white #FFFFFF, about 2.9% em, with the two-line labels set tight. Chip labels 'Intel® Gaudi® 2' and 'Intel® Gaudi® 3' are semibold sans-serif in cyan-blue #3FC8F0 to #1CA6E0 with the 2 and 3 in the same gradient, about 4% em; '7nm' and '5nm' are light sans-serif in light cyan #8FDFF2, about 2.9% em. Lines are thin: the timeline arrow is 2px-equivalent light cyan #8FE3F7, dotted connectors are small round dots about 3px. No shadows on text; chips have no borders or shadows apart from a faint glow.

Left edge carries decorative circuit and code graphics, and the Intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about x 7%, y 90%. No slide number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark blue background illustration with glowing circuit traces and a pixel-grid pattern at upper-left, a glowing blue chip outline with purple-red core at about x 10.5% to 22%, y 27.5% to 48%, and a red, cyan and white code-bar graphic at bottom-left. Three chip photographs: (1) Intel Gaudi 2 die shown as a gold/orange package with black substrate and a central die marked GAUDI2, at about x 25.3%, y 43.4%, w 12%, h 19.6%, sharp, no crop, boxed by its own black edges; (2) Intel Gaudi 3 dual-die package shown with pink-red and blue-tinted tiles and a black substrate, labelled GAUDI3, at about x 53.1%, y 41.5%, w 10.5%, h 23%; (3) Falcon Shores placeholder shown as a flat blue-gradient rectangle with a light-blue glowing border at about x 79.5%, y 40.3%, w 10.5%, h 23%. A thin light-cyan horizontal timeline arrow runs from about x 17.4% to 95.5% at y 68.7%, with a filled arrowhead at the right end and small upright tick marks at about x 31%, 58%, 85%.

Milestone labels in bold sans-serif, #3CC8F5 (cyan); 'Codename' and nanometre labels in light sans-serif, letter-spaced, #8ED9F2. Title about 9.5% of slide height, subtitle about 4.5%, milestone labels about 4.3%, sub-labels about 2.7%. Timeline line #7FDFFF, about 0.3% of slide height thick. Chip images have no rounded corners; Falcon Shores frame has a soft blue outer glow. Shadows: none on text.

Background periodic-table tiles, faint and partly cropped, include: Al Aluminium 26.981538; Si Silicon 28.0855; P Phosphorus 30.973762; S Sulfur 32.065; Se Selenium 78.96; As Arsenic 74.9216; Ge Germanium 72.64; Ga Gallium 69.723; Zn; Cu; Cd Cadmium 112.414; In Indium 114.818; Sn Tin 118.71; Sb Antimony 121.76; Te Tellurium 127.6; I Iodine 126.90447; Tl Thallium 204.38; Pb Lead 207.2; Bi Bismuth; Hg Mercury 200.59; Ag Silver 107.8682; Au Gold 196.966569; Os Osmium 190.23; Ir Iridium 192.217; Pt Platinum 195.084; Pd Palladium 106.42. Small circuit labels include INPUT CHECK, TRANSFER, ECHO, 2400, 2804, 0.4823.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue slide with the title Moore's Law at upper left, left-aligned, starting about 15.6% from the left and 10.8% from the top, and its light subtitle on the same baseline. A large boxed scatter chart fills the centre-left from about 13% to 75% of the width and 21% to 80% of the height, with y-axis labels to its left and x-axis year labels beneath it. A 3D chip illustration bleeds off the right edge, overlapping the chart's right side. The Intel innovation logo sits at bottom left, about 7% from the left and 90% from the top. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Scatter plot with logarithmic y-axis and linear x-axis. Title: none printed on the chart. Y-axis: Transistors Per Device, log scale, gridlines per decade, labelled from 1,000 at bottom to 1,000,000,000,000 at top. X-axis: years 1970 to 2030 at 10-year intervals, with plot range 1970 to 2030. Series 1: Intel and non-Intel CPU, shown as about 150 grey-white dots with about 70% opacity, values estimated: about 2,300 in 1971 (Intel 4004 point), about 3,500 in 1972, about 29,000 in 1974, about 4,500 in 1978, about 1,000,000 in 1989, about 3,000,000 in 1993, about 42,000,000 in 2000, about 220,000,000 in 2001, about 1,700,000,000 in 2006, about 7,000,000,000 in 2010, about 30,000,000,000 in 2019, about 39,000,000,000 in 2020 (estimates, no values printed). Series 2: 2X every 2 years, a straight cyan line from about 1,000 at 1970 to about 1,000,000,000,000 at 2030, running from the bottom-left corner to the top-right corner of the plot. Legend: upper-left inside the plot, with a white dot marker for Intel and non-Intel CPU and a cyan line marker for 2X every 2 years. No highlighted points. Photograph/illustration: right side, a glowing 3D render of a processor chip, bright blue silicon die with a central square cache pattern, surrounded by a copper-red glowing rim and light streaks, with rectangular blue heat-spreader tiles below. Lighting: strong blue and orange-red neon glows. Dominant colours: royal blue, cyan, deep navy, and orange-red highlights. The chip is cropped by the right edge of the slide, and the image blends into the periodic-table background with an overlay that darkens the edges. Logo: the Intel innovation wordmark, white text 'intel' in bold with a small registered mark, followed by 'innovati' in white and 'on' in cyan.

**Design**

Background is a deep gradient from bright royal blue #0A5CE0 at top left to deep navy #00114A at bottom. Title Moore's Law in bold geometric sans-serif (Intel One Display Bold style), cyan #2BC8F5 for Moore's Law and light grey-white #C9D6E8 for is alive and well, which is regular weight. Body text and axis labels: white #FFFFFF, Intel One Display Regular. Legend labels: Intel One Display Medium, white. Accents: cyan #1EC8FF for the trend line and the chart border. ### Slide 25

**Text**

AI PC / Fundamentally transforming / the PC experience / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed illustration fills the whole slide. A laptop sits on the left half, with circuit and dashboard graphics along the left and right edges. The text block sits left-aligned at about x 49% and is vertically centred on the slide's middle band, with the title above a two-line subtitle. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left edge. There is no footer, no page number and no visible margin frame.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed digital illustration covering the whole slide. A open laptop, angled slightly, sits in the lower-left-centre with its screen facing the viewer. The screen shows a dark-blue dashboard with pink and magenta bar-code-like code lines, wave/area charts in teal and green, a fingerprint icon, user-profile icons, a radar/target icon and a small dotted bar chart. Along the left edge are a column of circular glowing chip icons linked by thin cyan circuit traces, small meter and checklist cards, and a glowing red/pink LED-bar graphic at the top. On the upper right is a large ring gauge, green arc on a grey track, with a cyan outlined cloud inside. Further right are stacked rounded-rectangle cards with user-profile icons, a cloud icon, bar-style meters and pink line graphs, all with cyan outlines. Lighting is a soft cyan glow from the laptop base and screen. Dominant colours are royal blue, cyan #00C8FF, magenta #E0309A and navy #021A5E. The illustration is not cropped and has no boxed frame, and there is no tint over the text area.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue radial gradient, lighter royal blue #1450C8 at upper left and centre, fading to navy #021A5E at the bottom right. Title 'AI PC' is a bold geometric sans in a cyan-to-light-blue gradient, roughly #3ED8F8 to #5CB8F5, large at about 12% of slide height. Subtitle is a light-weight geometric sans with wide letter-spacing in pale cyan #7FE3F8, about 3.4% of slide height per line, with normal case. The logo is white 'intel' in a bold sans with 'innovation' in a regular sans, and the 'on' is cyan #00C7FD. Illustration uses thin cyan #00C8FF outlines, rounded corners on cards, and soft glow shadows around the LEDs and circuit lines. Gradients are used on the title and background.

### Slide 26

**Text**

intel CORE ULTRA / Age of the AI PC / Launch of Intel® Core™ Ultra / December 14, 2023 / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue photographic background. A tall silver chip package sits in the left third, with the Core Ultra badge overlapping its right edge. The title block sits in the right half, left-aligned at about x 50.7%, with the title, subtitle, a small white square and a red date box stacked vertically. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left and a circuit graphic runs along the bottom edge. No footer text, slide number or date beyond the date box.

**Visuals**

Photograph of an Intel Core Ultra processor package, shown upright. The silver metal lid frames a dark grey-blue substrate with a dotted pattern. The central die is a rectangle of multicoloured tiles, with blue tiles at the top, grey and gold tiles in the middle, and gold and tan tiles at the bottom. The package is lit from the front with soft white highlights on the metal edges. Composition is centred vertically, nearly full height, with its top and bottom edges close to the slide edges, so it is not cropped. Beside it is a dark navy Intel Core Ultra badge, slightly rotated in perspective, with a blue-to-navy gradient face, a white 'intel' wordmark, white 'CORE' letters, white 'ULTRA' capitals and a grid of small blue and cyan dots on its right side. A soft shadow sits under the badge. Behind the scene are blue abstract circuit and light-streak textures, pink and magenta bar-chart-like lines at the left edge, and a row of glowing cyan, pink and red dot-and-line circuit graphics along the bottom. A solid red-coral rectangle #FF5662 holds the date text. A small white square sits above its top-left corner. The intel innovation logo is white with a cyan 'on'.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient, #0B2A8F at upper left to #071B6B at right. Title is a bold geometric sans-serif in a cyan-to-blue gradient, #2DD4F5 to #3B82F6, sized about 9 percent of slide height. Subtitle is a light regular geometric sans-serif in cyan #5FD3F7, about 4 percent of slide height. Date text is white #FFFFFF in a regular geometric sans-serif (IntelOneDisplay-Regular), about 4.2 percent of slide height, centred in the red box. The red box is a sharp-cornered rectangle #FF5662 with no border or shadow. The small square is white #FFFFFF. Accent colours are cyan #2DD4F5, magenta #E0307A and red #FF5662. Imagery has no rounded corners, and the chip package has a soft drop shadow.

Lighting is a soft blue glow from below the laptop and faint light streaks behind it. Dominant colours are royal blue, cyan and pale grey-blue. The image covers the whole slide with no crop or box. Overlays: faint horizontal light streaks at upper left and a small cyan cloud line-art outline with a chevron near the bottom left. Logos: Microsoft four-colour square logo with Microsoft wordmark in white; Copilot icon (blue-to-purple interlocking hexagon ring) with Copilot text; a cyan plus sign; Intel wordmark in white with registered mark; Intel Innovation logo (neon chevron icon in red, orange and yellow with white intel and cyan innovation wordmark).

**Design**

Background: Blue gradient from #0A4FA8 at the top to #052A6B at the bottom-left, with a brighter #1E6BE0 glow behind the laptop, covered by a full-bleed image. Deep royal blue gradient background, darker navy at the bottom left and brighter blue around the laptop. Logos and text are white; the plus sign and cloud line-art are bright cyan #3FD0F5. The Copilot wordmark is set in a light regular sans-serif, about 2.7 percent of slide height. The Microsoft and Intel wordmarks are set in a bold-to-regular sans-serif, white. Glowing neon accents in cyan and magenta-red on the Intel Innovation logo. No corner radii or card borders on the slide itself; the laptop has rounded screen corners and a flat keyboard deck.

### Slide 28

**Text**

intel CORE ULTRA / First on Intel 4 / Using EUV Technology / Thunderbolt 4 / 3D Performance Hybrid Architecture / Built-In NPU AI Engine / Low Power Island E-cores / WiFi 7 / Foveros 3D Packaging / New P-core & E-core Microarchitectures / Built - in intel ARC / Latest Media & Display Standards / intel innovation

**Layout**

A full-bleed blue background with a 4-row-by-5-column irregular mosaic of rectangular tiles occupying the middle band of the slide, from about 9% to 82% of width and 29% to 83% of height. Tiles sit on a tight grid with roughly 0.3% gutters and no outer rounding. The Intel Core Ultra logo plaque sits top-left over the first row, with no conventional title; the bottom-left holds the intel innovation logo and the bottom-centre a circuit-line illustration. No slide number or footer text is printed.

**Visuals**

Photographs and illustrations, all rectangular and boxed (not full-bleed except the background): (1) Top-right isometric render of an Intel Core Ultra processor package, blue-tinted, layered tiles with glowing edges, spanning about x 52-100, y 2-75, partly behind tiles; (2) Left edge vertical colourful code/data bars (red, magenta, cyan) spanning about x 0-12, y 53-100; (3) Bottom-centre thin cyan and red line-art circuit with a chevron node and a glowing bar graph, about x 39-75, y 88-99; (4) Row 1 tile photo at x31.8 y29.4 w9.6 h17.2: black cube with silver blade structure and blue diagonal lines, lit from the upper right; (5) Row 1 tile photo at x51.6 y29.4 w19.4 h17.0: grey EUV lithography machine interior with bright violet-purple beams and reflective metal, dominant violet and grey; (6) Row 2 photo at x9.5 y47.2 w9.6 h16.9: close-up of a golden-lit chip die with a grid, dominant tan and dark brown, cropped to the tile; (7) Row 2 photo at x41.9 y47.2 w9.5 h17.0: 3D rendering of stacked blue-lit chip tiles on a circuit substrate with golden highlights; (8) Row 3 photo at x19.6 y64.9 w9.9 h17.3: close-up of copper-coloured solder bumps in a regular grid on dark grey, shallow depth of field; (9) Row 3 photo at x61.7 y65.1 w9.2 h17.1: silver metallic pins and a glass cylinder in greyscale with blue tint; (10) Logos: Thunderbolt 4 lightning-bolt mark in white at x74.0 y31.9 w4.0 h7.8; WiFi 7 mark (a stylised 7 inside wifi arcs) in white at x12.3 y69.6 w3.8 h6.8; intel ARC wordmark in white at x53.2 y73.5 w7.4 h5.0; intel innovation wordmark in white with cyan 'on' at x7.1 y90.2 w18.2 h7.7; Intel Core Ultra plaque logo at x4.2 y13.2 w11.2 h22.6. No overlays other than tile gradients; no shadows on photos.

**Design**

Background: Blue gradient from ultramarine #1A3FA0 to midnight #0B1F6B, with an isometric processor illustration and circuit textures at the right and left edges. Deep blue background, gradient from ultramarine #1A3FA0 at top-left to midnight #0B1F6B at bottom-right with a faint lighter band across the top at about y 4-6%. Tiles use a two-stop diagonal gradient: text tiles run from navy-blue #1E4D9A at left to magenta-rose #C8476B at bottom-right (First on Intel 4, 3D Performance Hybrid, NPU AI Engine, Foveros); smaller tiles run from royal blue #1B5FC0 at top to cyan-teal #0AA6D8 at bottom-right (Using EUV Technology, Thunderbolt 4, Low Power Island, New P-core, Built - in ARC, Latest Media & Display, WiFi 7). Text is white #FFFFFF throughout. Typeface is IntelOne Text, a geometric grotesque sans-serif, in Light and Medium weights; no capitals or letter-spacing. Title-size text is about 4.2% of slide height (First on Intel 4), body and tile headings about 2.9-3.3%, small captions about 2.1%, and the smallest about 1.5-1.9%. Shapes are sharp-cornered rectangles with no borders, no visible shadows, and thin 0.3% gaps between tiles.

### Slide 29

**Text**

acer / Jerry Kao / Chief Operating Officer, Acer / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed illustration fills the slide. A left-aligned text block sits at the centre-right: the acer logo, then the name Jerry Kao, then the title line. The Intel innovation logo sits bottom-left with a margin of about 7% from the left edge. There is no title placeholder, footer text or slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed digital illustration in a neon tech style. Left half: a glowing cyan outline cloud above a grid of pixels and glowing rows, an orange-pink ring with a light bloom, a smartphone with glowing edges and code lines on its screen, and an open laptop with a glowing screen showing code bars and a lit keyboard. Right edge: circuit-board motifs with a fingerprint icon, a circular chip-like icon, a square chip with a grid of cells, and cyan traces. Lighting is glowing neon cyan, magenta and orange against a deep blue field. The image covers the whole slide and is not cropped or boxed, with no tint overlay beyond the artwork. The acer logo, name and title line and Intel innovation logo are placed on top of the artwork.

**Design**

Background: Deep blue gradient, #0A4FC0 at the top fading to #00114A at the bottom, with the full-bleed neon illustration over it. Deep blue gradient background, brighter blue near the top (#0A4FC0) fading to near-navy at the bottom (#00114A). The acer logo is white #FFFFFF. **Visuals**

Illustration: a faint circuit-board graphic in cyan #29D3F5 lines and dark blue fills runs along the top-right corner, from about x 82% to the right edge and from the top to about y 52%. It contains a fingerprint icon in a rounded square outline, two circular chip or sensor icons linked by dotted green-cyan lines, and a large rounded-square chip outline in magenta-violet #C93FD8 with a grid of small blue and red squares and a white dot at its centre, with cyan traces leading off to the lower right. Photograph: an open silver laptop, seen from the front left, with its screen at about x 22-49% and y 8-60%, and its base at about y 62-88%. The screen shows an abstract close-up of a flower in purple, magenta, pink and electric blue petals on a black background. The keyboard is dark with purple-tinted keys, the trackpad is flat, and there is a small blue Acer logo on the bezel. The lighting is soft and bright from the front, with a cool blue cast on the metal. Full-colour, not cropped, not boxed; a soft shadow sits beneath the base. Logo: the acer wordmark in white, slanted italic lowercase, centred at about x 59-79%, y 33-41%. Plus sign: a thin cyan #3FC8F0 cross, centred at about x 68%, y 47%. Logo: the intel wordmark in white bold lowercase, with a small cyan #00C7FD square above the i, and a small registered mark, centred at about x 63-76%, y 57-65%. Logo: intel innovation lockup in white (intel in bold, innovation in regular) with the 'on' in cyan #00C7FD, bottom-left at about x 7-25%, y 90-98%.

**Design**

Background is a saturated blue gradient running from royal blue #1F5BE0 at the top left to deep navy #00104A at the bottom right, with a lighter steel-blue haze at the lower left around the laptop base. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF. Accents are cyan #3FC8F0 for the plus sign and the intel innovation 'on', and a magenta-pink band #C0408F at about 50-56% of height on the left edge, partly transparent. Typeface: the text is set in IntelOneDisplay, a clean geometric grotesque sans-serif; 'The Upcoming' and 'Thin & Powerful Laptop' are regular weight, and 'Acer Swift' is medium weight. Body text is about 3% of slide height; the 'Acer Swift' line is about 4.1% of slide height. Line and shape styles: the circuit traces are thin 1-2px cyan lines with rounded corners; the chip outline has rounded corners and a magenta glow. No drop shadows on text; the laptop has a soft ground shadow. No visible gradient overlays beyond the background haze.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Future Generations / of Intel Core Processors / Realize your vision with AI PC capabilities / 2024 / Arrow Lake / Lunar Lake / Panther Lake / 2025 / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue illustration with the title block in the upper centre-left, starting at about 27.5% of slide width, and the subtitle directly beneath it. A horizontal timeline runs across the lower middle (about 27% to 94% of width) with three processor chips above it, labelled below the line, and the years 2024 and 2025 at its ends. A laptop illustration fills the left edge (0 to 27% of width), and the intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left and 90% from the top. There is no slide number or footer text. Text is left-aligned, with the title and subtitle sharing one left edge.

**Visuals**

Full-slide digital illustration in a flat-to-glossy tech style. Left edge: a laptop, open and angled, showing a dashboard screen with a fingerprint icon, user-profile cards, line charts (green, blue and purple), bar-like data tables and a crosshair icon. Around the laptop are circular icon badges (dot-matrix circles, a glowing red-and-pink ring, a check-list icon) linked by thin cyan circuit lines, plus a row of pink and cyan LED bars at top left. Top right: a large circular ring gauge with a green arc and a grey arc, enclosing a glowing cyan outline cloud icon. Right edge: stacked dashboard panels with user icon, cloud icon and pink-red data bars. Centre: three processor packages on a timeline. The first (Arrow Lake) shows a detailed die photograph with blue and gold/orange circuit blocks. The second (Lunar Lake) and third (Panther Lake) show plain blue-grey gradient rectangles standing in for dies, each set in a dark slate package frame with a soft blue glow. Each package has a small white pin marker on the timeline below it. The timeline is a thin pale cyan line with a small arrow at its right end. Lighting is a soft blue glow behind the chips and along the laptop edges. Dominant colours: royal blue, deep navy and cyan, with pink and magenta accents. The illustration is full-bleed and not cropped, with no boxing or overlay other than a dark navy tint toward the bottom.

**Design**

Background: deep blue gradient, lighter royal blue #0A4FB0 at top fading to dark navy #001238 at bottom. Title: bold geometric sans-serif (close to Intel Clear Bold), cyan-to-light-blue gradient, approximately #3FD8F5 to #1FA8E8, about 6.6% of slide height, two lines with tight leading. Subtitle: light-weight sans-serif with wide letter-spacing, pale cyan #7FE3F7, about 3.2% of slide height. Timeline years: light sans-serif, white-cyan #CDEFFA, about 2.8% of slide height. Processor labels: semibold sans-serif in cyan gradient #35D0F4 to #1FA0E0, about 4.2% of slide height. Logo: white lowercase 'intel' in bold with 'innovation' in a regular weight, with the 'o' in cyan #22C7F0. Line style: timeline stroke 2px pale cyan #8FE6FA. Chip frames: rounded dark slate #1E3A6E with soft outer glow #3D7BE0. Shadows and gradients: soft glow around chips, no hard shadows.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Performance
per unit of energy
must become our industry’s mission
intel innovation

**Layout**

Two-zone composition: a left-aligned text block sits at about 8.5% from the left edge, vertically centred between roughly 35% and 58% of the slide height, with a dense circuit and code illustration filling the right 40% and bleeding off the right edge. The logo lockup sits in the bottom-left corner at about 7% from the left and 90% from the top. There is no title placeholder, footer, date or slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration covering the whole slide: on the right, a large 3D microchip or processor package (blue-grey body with a glowing rainbow edge running red, yellow, green and cyan along its right side) tilted diagonally, with a circuit-trace pattern across it. Above and behind the chip is a ring gauge, about 25% of slide width, with a green arc and dark navy track, containing a cloud outline icon. Three vertical stacked code-editor panels in the centre-right (about x 59% to 69%), each a rounded-corner rectangle with a cyan-teal border: the top panel shows red and pink code lines, the middle panel shows a purple-to-pink area chart above orange and magenta code lines, and the bottom panel shows purple and blue code lines. Below the bottom panel is a small green wave chart (about x 60% to 66%, y 85% to 90%) and a grey-outlined warning triangle with an exclamation mark. A small person icon sits in an outlined box at about x 70%, y 38%. Falling dotted teal pixel streams run from the top edge at about x 59% to 69%. Bottom-left logo: a neon red and orange double-chevron emblem inside a blue outlined tag, next to a white cloud icon with a blue arrow, followed by the white wordmark 'intel' with a small registered mark and 'innovation' in white with the final 'on' in cyan. Faint horizontal streaks run across the lower centre of the slide.

**Design**

Background: Deep blue gradient (#0056D2 top left to #001A6E bottom) with the circuit illustration over the right side. Deep blue gradient background, lighter cerulean #0056D2 at top left fading to midnight navy #001A6E at the bottom. ### Slide 34

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: small light captions on dark art on slides 12, 14, 34, 36 and 52 and the dense logo and member lists on 56 and 64 would be hard to read, and the chart on 23 has tiny axis labels.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small captions and sub-labels on slides 12, 34, 36, 52 and 62 and raise their contrast against the busy art, and simplify the member list on 56.

**Text**

intel. / XEON / Xeon Roadmap / Designed to deliver higher performance using / less energy measured as performance per watt / 5th Gen Intel® Xeon® / Formerly codenamed / Emerald Rapids / Next Gen Intel® Xeon® / Codenamed / Sierra Forest / Next Gen Intel® Xeon® / Codenamed / Granite Rapids / Next Gen Intel® Xeon® / Codenamed / Clearwater Forest

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue background with the title block in the upper centre-right, starting at about 32% from the left and 25% from the top. A horizontal timeline arrow runs across the lower middle at about 66% of the height, with four chip images above it and four labels below it, evenly spaced from left to right. The left third is occupied by a large circuit-board chip illustration and a cloud-ring icon. There is no footer, page number or logo bar.

**Visuals**

Left side: a large isometric illustration of a glowing processor die on a circuit-board background, with a cyan and magenta light gradient, cloud icons and coloured binary-style bar graphics at the bottom left. Top left: a circular ring icon with a cloud symbol. Upper centre: a 3D rendered Intel Xeon product box with the intel. logo and XEON wordmark in white on navy blue. Timeline: a thin light-blue horizontal arrow line with a filled triangular arrowhead at the right end, with a small vertical tick pin under each chip. Four chip images above the line: the first is a photograph of a gold-toned processor package (5th Gen, Emerald Rapids), and the other three are blue-tinted schematic renders of processor packages with glowing blue outlines (Sierra Forest, Granite Rapids, Clearwater Forest).

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient, bright cobalt #0B5FD0 at top left shading to navy #00124F at bottom right. Title colour is light cyan #5ADCF7 with a slight gradient toward #3AB8F0. Subtitle is light sky blue #7FD8F8. Milestone headings are bold in bright cyan #2FC3F2. Milestone sublines are lighter cyan-white #A8E6FA. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif with regular weight for the subtitle and sublines, semi-bold for the title and headings. Title text is about 5.5% of slide height; subtitle about 2.6%; milestone text about 2.2%. Timeline line is light blue #62D6F5 with a 0.2% stroke. Chip frames have soft blue glow shadows; no rounded corners on the timeline.

### Slide 35

**Text**

Launch of 5th Gen Intel® Xeon® Scalable Processor
Formerly codenamed Emerald Rapids
December 14, 2023

**Layout**

Full-bleed widescreen composition with the processor die photograph in the left-centre, a connector-tree graphic and the Intel Xeon badge in the centre, and a text block in the right third. The title block sits at about x 58 to 90 and y 46 to 60, left-aligned, with a red date box beneath it. There is no footer, page number or logo bar, and the margins are about 10% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration of a dark blue-to-violet gradient with glowing cyan outline cloud at top left, a grid of blue and cyan squares falling down the left edge, and a multicoloured bar-graph motif at bottom left (orange, red, magenta and cyan horizontal bars with a glowing vertical neon line and a small left-pointing chevron). Centre-left: a photograph of a 5th Gen Intel Xeon Scalable processor die viewed from above, in a dark teal circuit-board substrate with two large beige-gold tiles diagonally textured, boxed with no crop, with a small square chip at top right of the tile. Centre: a vertical blue dotted connector column with four stacked horizontal bar-chart motifs (cyan/purple, red/magenta/blue, red/orange and magenta/cyan) linked by thin cyan bracket lines to the processor. Upper-centre right: a 3D-rendered Intel Xeon product box in dark blue with the intel wordmark and XEON logotype in white, slightly angled. Right: no photograph. Lighting is a soft glow from the cyan and blue elements against a dark background. Dominant colours are electric blue, navy, violet, cyan and coral red.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient from bright blue #0A6FE0 at top to navy #101A5C at right and violet-black #2A0F4F at bottom left. Title text is bold sans-serif (Intel One Display style) in a cyan-to-blue gradient, around #3CC8F5 to #5A8CF0. Subtitle is light sans-serif in cyan #3FB8E8. Date text is white regular sans-serif with wide letter-spacing, on a coral-red box #FF5662. Accent colours are cyan #00D1FF and coral #FF5662. Title is about 4.6% of slide height, subtitle about 3.0%, date 3.7%. Shapes have square corners with no shadow; the small white square marker sits at the top-left corner of the date box. The processor and badge have soft drop shadows.
