### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: small light captions on dark art on slides 12, 14, 34, 36 and 52 and the dense logo and member lists on 56 and 64 would be hard to read, and the chart on 23 has tiny axis labels.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small captions and sub-labels on slides 12, 34, 36, 52 and 62 and raise their contrast against the busy art, and simplify the member list on 56.

**Text**

Xeon Roadmap
Designed to deliver higher performance using
less energy measured as performance per watt
5th Gen Intel® Xeon®
Formerly codenamed
Emerald Rapids
Next Gen Intel® Xeon®
Codenamed
Sierra Forest
Next Gen Intel® Xeon®
Codenamed
Granite Rapids
Next Gen Intel® Xeon®
Codenamed
Clearwater Forest
Birch Stream Platform Architecture
Based on architectural projections as of August 21, 2023 relative to prior generation. Your results may vary.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue technical background with a large chip illustration and a boxed Xeon product render on the left third. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at about 32.5% of slide width in the upper-middle area, above a horizontal timeline arrow spanning the lower-middle width with four chip images and their labels evenly spaced beneath it. A red bracket with a label marks the three rightmost generations below the labels. The footnote sits bottom-right, right-aligned to about 86.5% of width near the bottom margin. No slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background photograph/illustration: a dark-blue digital circuit scene with glowing cyan traces. Left side: a large 3D processor die (CPU chip) rendered in translucent blue with a rainbow glow (red, orange, yellow, green) along its left edge, with thin circuit lines extending right; a cloud icon at bottom-left and a cloud-in-ring icon at top-left; vertical glowing bars (white, pink, yellow) and pixel-grid/bar graphics at bottom-left. Top-centre-left: a 3D product render of an Intel Xeon processor package, dark navy box with the white 'intel' wordmark above a white 'XEON' wordmark, with a light-blue side edge, floating. Four chip images in a row along the timeline: first is a real photograph of a dark-green Emerald Rapids package with a gold/tan die; the other three are blue-tinted schematic renders of Sierra Forest, Granite Rapids and Clearwater Forest packages with glowing pale-blue die centres and faint glow halos. Timeline: a thin cyan horizontal line with a solid cyan arrowhead on the right, with a small white pin/tick under each chip connecting to the chip. Bracket: thin red-coral line forming a bracket beneath the three Next Gen labels, open at its centre for the label.

**Design**

Background: deep blue gradient from bright blue #0A5FD0 at top-left to dark navy #0A1F5C at centre and near-black navy #03103A at bottom-right. Title 'Xeon Roadmap' in bold sans-serif (Intel One Display Bold style) in cyan #5FD4F7 with a slight lighter gradient toward #7FE3F5. Subtitle in light sans-serif (Intel One Display Regular) in pale cyan #7ED9F7 with the second line slightly deeper blue-cyan #3FB8F0; subtitle letter-spacing slightly open. Labels: bold lines in bright cyan #3AC8FA ('5th Gen Intel® Xeon®', 'Next Gen Intel® Xeon®'); the second and third lines in light regular weight, mixed white #FFFFFF and pale blue #B8E6FA for 'Formerly codenamed' and 'Emerald Rapids'. Bracket label in coral red #FF5662 with wide letter-spacing. Footnote in small white regular sans-serif. Timeline line cyan #3FD0FF, about 0.3% of height thick, with a solid triangular arrowhead. Chip glows in soft cyan. Title about 6% of slide height; subtitle lines about 3.3%; labels about 2.4%; footnote about 1.1%. No rounded corners or shadows on text; chip glows act as soft outer shadows.

**Layout**

The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left at about x 7 to 25, y 90 to 98. There is no title placeholder at the top, no page number and no footer text.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style render of a terminal window: a dark charcoal (#1C1C1C) panel with a thin cyan outline, spanning about x 8.3 to 54.4 and y 36.2 to 83.4. Inside, monospaced light grey text on the charcoal, with the lines 'processor : 559' through 'processor : 575' stacked vertically, then a prompt 'demo@srfubuntu22p4-3405:~$' followed by a light grey block cursor. The top lines fade toward the top edge. A small white mouse pointer sits at the left edge of the list near line 568. A blue header band (cyan #00A6E0 at left fading to royal blue #1A4E9A at right) sits above the terminal at about x 8.3 to 54.3, y 24.3 to 36.5, containing the title 'Sierra Forest demo' and subtitle '2 processors with 288 cores'. A 3D-rendered Intel Xeon processor chip stands at about x 40.9 to 66.9, y 10.8 to 62.8, tilted slightly; its top face is dark navy with a white 'intel' wordmark at top-left and a large white-to-silver 'XEON' wordmark with a registered mark, and its side and base are mid blue (#1E4F8F) with a lighter blue edge. Cropping: none, the chip is a cut-out with no box. Lighting: a soft cyan glow behind the top-centre of the slide and a speckled light reflection on the chip. Dominant colours: deep blue, cyan, charcoal. The terminal panel overlaps the chip's lower-left base. The Intel Innovation logo is white 'intel' with a bold wordmark and 'innovation' in white with the final 'o' in cyan (#00C7FD), bottom-left. On the right edge there is a faint blurred horizontal streak of orange, yellow and violet light at about x 90 to 100, y 15 to 85, plus soft horizontal light lines running across the right side at about y 40 to 65.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue radial gradient: bright azure #0A6FD6 at top-centre, fading to navy #0A1F6B and midnight #001A4D at the edges and bottom. Headline text is bright cyan-blue #1EA7F0 for '288 cores' with a gradient to lighter cyan #6FD3F5 on the word 'cores'; 'Intel® Xeon® with E-core' is cyan #22B8F0 gradient to teal #3CC7D8; 'Launch 2024' is light cyan #5BC8F2 in a light weight. Subtitle and header text are white #FFFFFF. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (close to Intel One Display), headline bold, 'Launch 2024' light weight. Headline '288 cores' cap height is about 7.6 percent of slide height; subtitle 'Intel® Xeon® with E-core' about 4.4 percent; 'Launch 2024' about 3.8 percent. Terminal text is a monospaced serif-like font (close to Courier), regular weight, light grey #D0D0D0. Terminal panel has square corners and a thin cyan (#1FA8E0) 1 px border. Header band has square corners. No shadows except a soft shadow beneath the chip.

### Slide 38

Security / Exponential compute creates / a vastly broader attack surface

The title 'Security' is left-aligned at about 50% x, with the two-line subtitle beneath it; no footer, no slide number visible, no logos.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed digital illustration: a glowing cyan-outlined shield with a padlock icon centred inside a circular ring of arrows (two curved arrows with arrowheads, circular dashed guide rings) at left-centre. Surrounding circuit-board traces, a cloud outline at top-left and a cloud icon inside a rectangular frame at top-centre, a small cloud icon on a tile at bottom-left, stacked square pixel grids (top-left and bottom-left), vertical and horizontal code-like bar blocks in magenta, orange and blue, chevron arrow icons at bottom-right and bottom-centre, a glowing pink-and-orange chevron tile at bottom-right, and thin neon circuit lines with node dots throughout. Lighting is glowing neon; dominant colours are royal blue, cyan and magenta-pink accents. The illustration is full-bleed and not cropped in a box; no photograph, chart or table.

**Design**

Background: Deep blue gradient (about #0A2A6B at top-left shading to #04123A at bottom-right) with the illustration across the whole slide.

### Slide 39

**Text**

intel® / security / Security starts with intel / Learn more in Day 2 keynote

**Layout**

Full-bleed illustration covers the whole slide with no title placeholder, footer, or slide number. The text block sits in the right half, left-aligned at about x 50%, stacked as the intel security logo (top, about y 31–48%), the tagline (about y 55–61%), and the subtitle (about y 62–66%). The left half is occupied by the central shield graphic.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed flat vector illustration of a cybersecurity theme. Left: a large cyan outlined cloud at top-left (about x 0–19%, y 4–26%) with a pixel-grid block field beneath it. Centre-left: a large circular ring (about x 13–39%, y 30–76%) in cyan #2EC4F0 with a glossy highlight at its upper right, containing a shield outline with a padlock icon in the centre; two circular arrows run around the ring, one with an arrowhead at upper left and one at lower right, and a dashed outer circle surrounds it. Thin cyan circuit traces with dot nodes run from the ring outward to the right and top. Top centre: a small framed cloud icon (about x 25–35%, y 16–25%) and a red-pink LED strip panel (about x 47–59%, y 0–8%) and a magenta-outlined chip box at top right (about x 49–59%, y 0–8%). Code-line bars in magenta, blue and cyan appear at top centre (about x 38–44%, y 12–27%). Bottom-left: a code-editor-style panel with orange, red-pink and cyan horizontal bars (about x 8–16%, y 78–97%) and a small cyan cloud at left edge. Bottom-right: horizontal glowing blue light bands (about x 80–100%, y 76–95%), a red-outlined chevron-arrow tile with orange and red double chevrons (about x 91–99%, y 83–92%), and a framed panel with a fingerprint-like icon at bottom right (about x 82–100%, y 92–100%). Chevron arrows point left at about x 3%, y 86% and x 81%, y 87%. Lighting is even and glowing neon, with no people or photography. Dominant colours are deep royal blue, cyan, magenta, and orange accents.

**Design**

Background: Dark blue gradient (royal blue #1537A8 to navy #071A5E) with full-bleed circuit-board illustration. Dark blue gradient background from royal blue #1537A8 at top left to deep navy #071A5E at bottom right, with a subtle lighter glow at upper left. Logo wordmark is white #FFFFFF for 'intel' and cyan #29C5F6 for 'security'. The tagline 'Security starts with intel' is set in a bold geometric sans (Intel Clear Bold style), with a white-to-cyan gradient running from 'Security' to 'intel' (#FFFFFF to #3FD0F0). The subtitle is a light geometric sans (Intel Clear Light style) with wide letter-spacing in light cyan #8FE3F7. Illustration accents: cyan #29C5F6, magenta #E0357A, orange #F5A25A, blue #2D6BF0. Line styles are thin 1–2 px cyan traces with round node dots. Shapes have sharp corners with slight rounding on the chip panels, and a soft outer glow on neon elements. The tagline is roughly 5% of slide height and the subtitle roughly 3% of slide height.

### Slide 40

**Text**

Compute
Connectivity
Infrastructure
Artificial
Intelligence
Sensing
Technology Superpowers

**Layout**

Five equal square-ish tiles sit in a single horizontal row across the upper-middle of the slide, separated by thin white multiplication-style crosses; the row spans roughly 13% to 91% of the width. The title 'Technology Superpowers' and subtitle 'Enabled by Siliconomy' are left-aligned in a block at the right-centre below the tiles, starting near x 47%. A dense isometric city and circuit illustration fills the lower-left and background; no footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Five tiles, each a blue gradient square with a white line icon and white label: (1) Compute, icon of a processor chip inside a bracket frame; (2) Connectivity, icon of a hexagon of nodes with a wireless-signal arc in the centre; (3) Infrastructure, icon of a cloud above a network tree of three nodes; (4) Artificial Intelligence, icon of a microchip with circuit traces and 'AI' text in the centre; (5) Sensing, icon of an eye with concentric signal rings in the iris. Between tiles 1-2, 2-3, 3-4 and 4-5 are thin white X shapes. Background behind tiles: full-bleed background image of a dark navy-to-violet digital scene with glowing cyan and blue circuit traces at top left and top centre, a glowing cyan cloud icon in an outlined frame at top left, a pixel-grid cluster at top centre, and a neon cloud and red chevron icon in outlined frames at bottom centre. Lower-left: isometric city of blue, lavender and magenta extruded towers with lit windows on a faceted circular platform, lit from within, with a pink-magenta glow on the central tower. Composition is full-bleed with the illustration cropped at the bottom and left edges.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy-to-violet gradient, roughly #0A1F6B at right fading to #3A0F5C magenta at left edge, full-bleed digital circuit illustration over it.

### Slide 41

**Text**

Rewind / Dan Siroker / Founder of Rewind.ai / Search anything you've seen, said, or heard / Nov 1 9:41:00 AM / 9:35 AM / 9:40 AM / 9:45 AM / 9:55 AM / Find / # design / Today / brett / Paul Stamatiou / Yes same / Sounds good to me! You're the best. / Message #design / Landing page explorations / Assets / Page 1 / Rewind leverages the po... / Frame 1 to Frame 12 / Image 55

**Layout**

Full-bleed single-panel title slide with no title placeholder in the usual sense. The logo lockup sits left of centre at roughly 7 to 39% width, with the name block 'Dan Siroker' and 'Founder of Rewind.ai' stacked beneath it, left-aligned at about 16% width. A laptop mockup fills the right half (about 43 to 93% width, 20 to 82% height) and a coloured code graphic runs down the left edge. No footer, page number or logos beyond the Rewind mark.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style digital illustration of a silver-pink laptop with a blue base, shown open and seen from the front. The laptop screen displays the Rewind app: a search bar at the top ('Search anything you've seen, said, or heard'), a floating Slack window in the centre showing the #design channel with a short message thread (brett, Paul Stamatiou, Yes same, Sounds good to me! You're the best.), a macOS desktop behind it with a Dock containing Finder, Safari, Mail, Linear-style, Slack and Figma icons, a Figma window on the right ('Landing page explorations' with a Frames/Assets list), and a timeline strip along the bottom with times 9:35 AM, 9:40 AM, 9:45 AM and 9:55 AM and a playhead labelled 'Nov 1 9:41:00 AM'. The screen is framed by a dark bezel with a notch at the top. The laptop is lit by cyan glow and is set against a deep blue backdrop. Left edge: a vertical neon code visualisation of horizontal bars in orange, red, pink, violet and cyan with glowing white, cyan and yellow vertical light lines. Bottom: neon line-art decorations in cyan and blue, including a cyan outline cloud at about 51 to 59% width, a small left-pointing cyan chevron to its left, a dotted green and red path, and a glowing hexagonal tag with an orange and yellow '>>>' glyph at about 38 to 45% width. Composition: full-bleed, centred on the laptop, no crop of the laptop. Lighting: glowing neon from below the laptop and from the screen. Dominant colours: royal blue, violet, magenta-pink, cyan, white.

**Design**

Background: Blue-to-violet gradient (#2B3BB8 at top, #0B1F6B at bottom) with a violet-pink glow (#9A5BD8) in the top right. Deep blue-to-violet background gradient with a soft violet-pink glow in the top right corner. The Rewind wordmark is set in a heavy rounded geometric sans in a silver-white to lavender gradient (#E6E6F2 to #B9B6D8). The 'Rewind' icon is a pair of left-pointing play triangles in purple gradient (#9B4DFF to #5B2BD9). 'Dan Siroker' is set in a bold geometric sans with a cyan-to-blue gradient (#4FD8F5 to #2E8FE0). 'Founder of Rewind.ai' is a light, widely tracked sans in pale cyan (#6FE0F7). Title cap height is about 8% of slide height; name text about 7%; subtitle about 4%. Laptop bezel is dark grey; keyboard and base are blue (#1F6FE5) with cyan key glow (#3FD2FF). Neon elements have soft outer glows, no shadows or corner radii on text.

### Slide 42

**Text**

Rewind
+
intel®

**Layout**

Centred composition with the Rewind lockup and Intel logo stacked in a left-of-centre column, separated by a small cyan plus sign, and a laptop illustration occupying the right half. No title placeholder, footer, or slide number is visible. The full-bleed background illustration is the base layer and the logos sit on its dark-blue left area.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration of deep blue and violet gradient with glowing neon elements. Left edge: vertical stacks of neon code-editor bars in red, orange, yellow, cyan and purple, with white and cyan vertical glow lines. Right: a blue-and-cyan laptop illustration with its screen showing a pink-to-violet gradient wallpaper, a dark search bar reading 'Search anything you've seen, said, or heard', and a screenshot of a Slack-style #design channel window with chat messages, a timeline bar at the bottom reading 'Nov 1 9:41:00 AM' and times 9:35 AM, 9:40 AM, 9:45 AM, 9:55 AM. A dock of app icons is shown under the screenshot. Bottom: a cyan outline cloud icon, a cyan chevron, a yellow-orange double-chevron inside a blue outlined tag, and dotted green and red connector lines. Rewind logo: purple-to-violet double-left-triangle icon followed by the wordmark 'Rewind' in bold lavender-grey. Cyan thin plus sign. Intel logo: white 'intel' wordmark with a cyan square dot above the 'i' and a small ® mark.

**Design**

Background deep indigo-blue #2B2FB8 at top-left shading to violet-magenta #B25BD6 at top-right and deep navy #0B1A55 at bottom-left, with a cyan glow #2EC8F2 under the laptop. Wordmark 'Rewind' in lavender-grey #D6D4E8 with a subtle light gradient; Rewind icon purple gradient #7B3FE4 to #B06CF0. Intel wordmark white #FFFFFF; cyan accent #2EC8F2 for the plus sign and the Intel square. Typeface for 'Rewind' is a bold geometric humanist sans-serif, cap height about 8 percent of slide height. No body text. Lines are thin cyan strokes; laptop has rounded corners and cyan key glow; no visible drop shadows beyond the glow.

Logos: the intel ignite wordmark (top left), a square cloud-and-network icon, the ANTARIS wordmark with a satellite-over-arc emblem and the tagline SOFTWARE FOR SPACE, and the intel innovation wordmark (bottom left). Headline colours run from light cyan #4FD8F7 to bright cyan #00C6FC with a teal #3AD6EE shift on the last line. Logo wordmarks are white #FFFFFF, with the intel ignite word ignite in cyan #00C6FC. ### Slide 46

_Long standard legal text on this slide is summarised, not transcribed; its exact words are in the measured list below._

**Text**

intel + arm / “Ensuring that developers have access to leading-edge tools and capabilities that allow them to write once and deploy anywhere is a key focus for Arm. We are pleased to see initiatives such as OpenVINO which specifically target the deployment of complex AI models to Arm CPUs and enable critical cross-platform support as more intelligent compute moves to the edge.” / Dermot O'Driscoll / Vice President of Product Solutions, / Infrastructure Line of Business, Arm / intel innovation

**Layout**

Single-slide quote layout on a full-bleed dark background with the text block left-aligned on a left-hand column starting at about 9.6% of slide width. The intel + arm logo lockup sits above the quote at about 22% from the top, the quote runs in seven lines, and the attribution (name in bold, then two title lines) sits below it. The illustration fills the right third of the slide and is not text. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 8% margin. There is no title placeholder, slide number or footer text. The legal text is summarised: none on this slide.

**Visuals**

Right-hand illustration spanning roughly x 54 to 100 and y 0 to 100, in a neon cyan, blue, pink and green technical style. Elements: a glowing cyan outline of a cloud at top (x about 64 to 86, y 0 to 17) with a column of small blue squares falling from its base like rain (x about 66 to 83, y 17 to 53), tapering into a pixel grid. Pink and teal horizontal code-like bars stacked in the upper right edge (x about 73 to 100, y 0 to 40) and a second block of bars at mid-right (x about 86 to 100, y 40 to 80). A translucent blue square panel with a glowing cyan AI icon (a stylised brain or chip with circuit lines and arrows, x about 76 to 92, y 44 to 72) overlaps a dark blue laptop outline (x about 80 to 100, y 40 to 86) whose keyboard is drawn as fine lines at the bottom. At the bottom edge: a small cyan cloud outline with a left-pointing chevron (x about 82 to 89, y 84 to 90), a pink and orange double-chevron tag inside a blue outlined box (x about 70 to 76, y 90 to 97), green dotted lines with a green dot at right (x about 60 to 100, y 88 to 95), and a blue arrow-chevron at x about 60 y 94. Cropping: illustration bleeds off the right and bottom edges. Lighting: self-lit neon glow on a dark field. Dominant colours: cyan #19E6F5, electric blue #1E6BE6, pink #FF4D8D, green #3DDC84. Two translucent quotation-mark glyphs in pale blue gradient sit at the start of the quote.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed gradient from bright blue #0A78E6 at top-left through royal blue #0B3FA8 to deep navy #000A3C at bottom-right, with the neon illustration on the right. Dark navy to bright blue background gradient with no border. Quote text is white #FFFFFF in a light geometric sans-serif (Poppins or Outfit Light style), approximately 3.3% of slide height, with the highlighted words OpenVINO which specifically target the deployment of complex AI models to Arm CPUs in semibold coral-pink #FF9E9E. Attribution name Dermot O'Driscoll is bold geometric sans in cyan #4FD8F5 at about 3.4% of slide height. Title lines are light geometric sans in sky blue #5CC6F0 at about 2.9% of slide height. Accent colour: cyan #19E6F5 used for the plus sign and the logo's cyan letters. Quote glyphs are pale blue #4AA8F0 at about 50% opacity with a soft gradient. Line spacing about 3.8% of slide height between quote lines. Illustration uses thin 1 to 2 px neon lines with soft glow, no hard shadows, and semi-transparent blue panels.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue slide. Title 'Hybrid AI' with subtitle sits top-left at about 6% from the left edge, with the title baseline at about 17% of the height. The main diagram is a centred stack in the left two-thirds, running top to bottom: two rows of framework logos, a bracket into the OpenVINO hub, five hardware labels with arrows to Intel product badges, and an OS line; the Intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left and 90% from the top. The right third is a decorative illustration with no text. No page number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram (top to bottom). Row 1 of framework logos: Bloom, dolly, LLaMA 2 Meta, GPT-J, Open-LLaMA, LoRa. Row 2: TensorFlow, Paddle (飞桨), PyTorch, Caffe, ONNX, mxnet, Keras. Below the logos, a cyan bracket line with 7 vertical ticks (one per logo column) joins at a centre point and a short arrow points down into the OpenVINO logo. OpenVINO logo: white 'OpenVINO' wordmark with the 'O' glyphs drawn as concentric eyes and a TM mark, with 'Optimized Performance' below in cyan. Below it, five labels CPU, iGPU, GPU, NPU, FPGA are joined by a cyan horizontal line with five downward arrows to nine Intel product badges in a row: CORE (blue), ATOM (light blue), XEON (dark slate), GAUDI (navy), DATA CENTER GPU (blue), IRIS Xe MAX GRAPHICS (blue with red strip), ARC GRAPHICS (dark blue), MOVIDIUS (blue-violet), FPGA AI Suite (dark blue with blue wave graphic). Below them a cyan horizontal line with a downward arrow centred, leading to the text 'Linux Windows macOS'. Right-side illustration: a glowing cyan outline cloud at top with a column of pixel-dot rain falling from it, a blue-cyan pixel grid and bar-chart-like streaks on the far right edge, a translucent blue rectangle containing a cyan outline brain-chip icon, a blue laptop with a glowing screen and keyboard, a small cyan cloud with a left chevron, and a chevron-in-box icon with green dotted circuit lines at bottom right. Lighting is an internal glow of cyan and blue; no photographs.

**Design**

Background: deep gradient from purple-magenta #5A2A8A at top-left through navy #0B1E6B to near-black navy #030B3A at bottom-right, with a brighter blue glow #0A6FD8 at top centre. Title colour cyan-blue #2FB5EE (a light blue-to-cyan tone). Subtitle and body text white #FFFFFF. Accents cyan #1BC6F0 for connector lines, arrows, OpenVINO subtitle and the intel innovation 'on'. Typefaces: title is a heavy geometric sans (Intel One Display Bold style, closest generic geometric sans bold); subtitle and labels are a light geometric sans (Intel One Display Light style) with wide letter-spacing on labels. Title cap height about 7.4% of slide height (font about 10.5% of slide height); subtitle about 2.9%; labels about 2.5%; OS line about 2.5%. Lines are 0.3% of slide height thick, flat, no shadows; arrowheads are small filled triangles. Right-side illustration has glowing neon outlines with soft outer glow and translucent blue fills.

### Slide 48

**Text**

Project Strata / the development of a modular edge-native / software platform that will accelerate the / adoption of AI-Automation at the Edge / Observe / Orchestrate / Onboard / intel innovation

**Layout**

Single centred text-and-diagram stack on a full-bleed illustrated background. The title block is left-aligned at about x 19.5% and sits in the upper-middle band (y 21.8% to 35%). Three equal-width full-width-of-column boxes (x 19.1%, w 43.6%) stack vertically from y 44.3% to 65% with small arrows between them. The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left at about x 7%, y 90%. There is no footer, page number or slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a three-tier vertical stack of rectangles, bottom to top Onboard, Orchestrate, Onboard-to-top order as follows. Bottom box labelled Onboard (red-to-pink gradient, x 19.1%, y 67.3%, w 43.6%, h 9.7%). Middle box labelled Orchestrate (purple-to-lilac gradient, x 19.1%, y 55.7%, w 43.6%, h 9.7%). Top box labelled Observe (dark navy-blue to cyan gradient, x 19.1%, y 44.3%, w 43.6%, h 9.7%). Two small upward-pointing arrowheads with thin shafts connect the boxes: a pink arrow from Onboard up to Orchestrate (about x 40.8%, y 63.3%, w 1.8%, h 3.6%) and a lavender arrow from Orchestrate up to Observe (about x 40.8%, y 52%, w 1.8%, h 3.5%). Right side illustration: isometric dashboard panels (charts, bar graphs, pie charts, tables, and the labels Robert Ross and 43%) stacked on a glowing blue platform bar, with rows of dashed cyan connection lines. Left and top-right illustration: isometric stacked server racks with glowing cyan tops, and flat isometric circuit chips with concentric rings on the lower right and centre. Lighting is a soft cyan glow; dominant colours are electric blue, cyan and magenta-pink accents; the image is full-bleed with no crop or box.

**Design**

Background: Deep blue gradient from #0A56C4 at top to #00104A at bottom, with an isometric technology illustration over it. Full-bleed dark blue background with an isometric technology illustration. Title in bold geometric sans-serif (Intel Clear / Red Hat Display style), cyan gradient from #3FD0F5 to #7FE6FF. Subtitle in a light-weight geometric sans-serif with wide letter-spacing, pale cyan #6FD8F0. Box labels in semibold geometric sans-serif, white to pale cyan. Title cap height about 4.5% of slide height (em about 6.5%); subtitle em about 3.2%; box labels em about 5%. Box shapes are square-cornered with no shadow; gradients run left to right. Arrow strokes are thin, lavender #C8B0E8 and pink #F2A0B0.

### Slide 49

**Text**

FIT MATCH.ai® / Meera Bhatia / Chief Operating Officer, / Fabletics

**Layout**

Full-bleed single slide with a left-aligned text column starting at about 9.4% from the left edge and spanning roughly 39% to 68% of the slide height, vertically centred. The logo sits above the name, followed by the job title on two lines. The illustration fills the right and centre of the slide and runs off the right and bottom edges. No footer, page number or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed illustration of a technology and e-commerce scene on a dark blue background. Centre: a cyan outlined cloud at the top, with a pixel-dot column falling from it onto a teal-framed browser window showing a small shoe thumbnail, text lines and a teal pill button with a shopping-cart icon. Left of centre: a small translucent browser window with a dot-grid image, a pair of teal and lavender sneakers, and a stack of three faded tank tops with one solid lavender V-neck tee bearing a white diamond motif in front. Lower centre: a purple glowing microchip outline with a grid of red and white dots, and a circuit trace running from the bottom centre toward the chip. Lower right of centre: a translucent purple-blue wrap-style sport sunglasses icon. Right: a translucent pale blue wireframe humanoid male figure standing front-facing, full body, cut off at the feet near the bottom edge. Far right: vertical dot-matrix columns in cyan and green, and stacked code-panel strips in pink, red, blue and magenta running off the right edge with cyan connector lines. Small white upward chevrons sit between the figure and the browser window. Lighting is a soft glow from the centre-right. Dominant colours are navy #0A1F6B, royal blue #1E4FD8, cyan #29C5F6 and magenta #D6337F accents. Nothing is cropped except the code panels and the figure's feet.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from bright blue #0A6BE0 at the top right to deep navy #0A1A5C at the bottom, with faint horizontal light streaks across the top left and lower centre. Logo wordmark is white #FFFFFF in a bold geometric sans, with a two-tone blue-to-lavender capsule icon between FIT and MATCH. Name is set in a semibold geometric sans in light cyan #3CC8F2 with the surname in a lighter aqua #7FDDF5. Job title is a light regular geometric sans in pale cyan #7FD9F2 with wide letter-spacing. Title text is about 4.6 to 6.5% of slide height, subtitle about 3.4 to 3.6%. No shadows, borders or corner radii on text. The illustration uses flat shapes with soft glows and translucent overlays.

### Slide 50

**Text**

FIT MATCH.ai®
+
intel®
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed illustration fills the whole slide with no title or body text. A stacked logo lockup sits in the left third: the FIT MATCH.ai wordmark at about 40% height, a cyan plus sign, then the intel wordmark below it. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 90% height, with no visible footer, slide number or margin text.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed digital illustration covering the entire slide. Dark navy-to-royal-blue background with cyan light streaks at top-left. A glowing cyan outline cloud at upper centre sends a column of blue pixel squares downward, ending in a browser-style window with a teal product thumbnail, text lines and a teal pill-shaped shopping-cart button. Floating elements: a small translucent browser card with a product thumbnail at upper left, a pair of teal sneakers, a stack of translucent purple T-shirts with a white diamond-pattern shirt in front, and translucent purple sunglasses at lower centre. A glowing purple microchip with a white centre dot sits at lower centre with cyan traces. A translucent pale-blue wireframe standing human figure occupies the right third. A blue pixel-grid double helix stands between the figure and the far right, and stacked code panels in cyan, magenta and red run along the far right edge. Three small white upward chevrons sit beside the figure. Cyan circuit traces with a small red LED strip curve across the bottom centre. Lighting is a soft blue glow with neon accents; dominant colours are navy, royal blue, cyan, teal and magenta.

**Design**

Background is a navy-to-royal-blue gradient, brightest at the top-right corner. Logo wordmarks are white. Cyan #29C5F6 accents for the plus sign and illustration glows. Typefaces: the FIT MATCH.ai wordmark is a heavy geometric sans-serif in white, the intel wordmark is a bold sans-serif in white, and the intel innovation line pairs bold 'intel' with a light sans-serif 'innovation' whose 'on' is cyan. No body text. Logo heights are about 6% to 18% of slide height. Shapes are flat with soft glows, no hard borders and no shadows on the logos.

ignite

Full-bleed abstract photographic background of flowing light ribbons: cyan and electric-blue streaks sweep from the lower left and across the bottom, shifting to magenta and orange-pink streaks at the lower right, over a dark navy field. Soft translucent purple and blue veils fill the upper middle. The Intel ignite logo (white 'intel' with registered mark, cyan 'ignite') and the DEEP RENDER logo (black pixel-style letters on a pale lavender-white rectangle) are the only logos besides the bottom-left Intel innovation wordmark (white 'intel' with cyan 'on'). Award text uses cyan #3FCBEE to pale aqua #7FDCF2 gradient on 'Award Winner' and light cyan #34C3E8 on the smaller line. ### Slide 52

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: small light captions on dark art on slides 12, 14, 34, 36 and 52 and the dense logo and member lists on 56 and 64 would be hard to read, and the chart on 23 has tiny axis labels.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small captions and sub-labels on slides 12, 34, 36, 52 and 62 and raise their contrast against the busy art, and simplify the member list on 56.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photographic background with the title block centred horizontally in the upper-middle band, a row of eight equal square tiles across the middle of the slide sitting on a horizontal timeline, and milestone captions hanging beneath the tiles, all left-aligned to the tile grid on the left half and centred on each tile on the right. The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left edge; there is no footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed blue photograph of a close-up of a computer processor and circuit board, seen at a diagonal from upper-left to lower-right, with a dark blue overlay that darkens the bottom and right areas. Eight square tiles in a horizontal row: five white-to-light-grey gradient tiles labelled Intel 7, Intel 4, Intel 3, Intel 20A and Intel 18A, and three flat grey translucent tiles labelled Next, Next + and Next ++. Each tile is joined to a horizontal cyan timeline bar by a short vertical stub ending in a small pale square node. Captions under the tiles are the milestone labels listed in text. No chart or table; the timeline is the only diagram.

**Design**

Background is a deep royal blue gradient, lighter blue #0A5BD6 at top-left fading to navy #0A1E6E at bottom-right, with a darkened photograph overlay. Title colour is a gradient from bright cyan #3ED6FF to teal #2AB8E0. Subtitle is light cyan #5BDDF7 in a wide-tracked light sans. Intel tile labels are navy #0C1465. Milestone headers are cyan #4DE0FF, description text is pale cyan #A6EEFF. Typefaces are IntelOne Display (geometric sans-serif): Medium for the title, Medium for tile names and milestone headers, Light for numbers and descriptions with generous letter-spacing. Title is about 7.6% of slide height; subtitle about 3.0%; tile numerals about 4.5%; tile names about 2.5%; milestone headers about 2.9%; descriptions about 1.7%. Tiles have sharp square corners with a soft shadow; the timeline is a 0.4% tall line with a light gradient.

### Slide 53

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The process roadmap uses a simple staged layout that makes the timeline readable at a glance.

**Text**

Release the 0.9 PDK
to foundry customers
Test chips
for multiple programs
Ribbon FET and PowerVia
continuously evolving
process technology
High-NA EUV technology
development with 18A,
production with “Intel Next”
Beyond 5 Nodes in 4 Years
Looking into the future today
Intel
18A
Next
Next +
Next ++
On Track
Silicon goes
into the fab in
Q1 2024
intel innovation

**Layout**

Two-part slide on a full-bleed blue background. The left half holds a boxed 2x4 grid inside a thin cyan frame, with four text tiles in the left column and four images in the right column, each row aligned at the same height. The right half has a title and subtitle at the top, a horizontal timeline with four node boxes above a light cyan line, and an 'On Track' caption below the line; the Intel innovation logo sits bottom-left under the frame. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in the right column of the frame, from top to bottom: (1) a close-up of a blue-tinted semiconductor die with rows of transistor structures, lit with cool light and cyan and violet highlights, filling its box; (2) a dark blue abstract surface with the white Ericsson logo (three stacked slanted bars and the word ERICSSON) and the white arm wordmark on the right, over a faint hexagonal texture; (3) a dark close-up render of a ribbon FET transistor structure with thin blue-violet lines and grey metal blocks on black; (4) a grey machine interior with a glowing purple-violet EUV light cone, cropped at the bottom of the frame. Timeline: a horizontal light cyan line across the right half with four small square connector ticks dropping from the node boxes. Four square node boxes sit above the line: the first, 'Intel 18A', is a near-white glossy box, and the other three ('Next', 'Next +', 'Next ++') are translucent light grey boxes with navy text. The Intel innovation logo is a white 'intel' wordmark with a small registered mark, followed by 'innovation' where the final 'on' is cyan.

**Design**

Background is a blue-tinted photograph of a chip or circuit surface, dark navy at the right and bottom and lighter cyan-blue at the upper left. Title 'Beyond 5 Nodes in 4 Years' is a medium-weight geometric sans (IntelOne Display style) in white fading to light cyan #7FE3F5 toward 'Years'; subtitle 'Looking into the future today' is light-weight sans in cyan #3FC8E8 with wide letter-spacing. Tile text is white (#FFFFFF) in IntelOne Display Medium for headings and Light for sub-lines, with the tile headings at about 3.0 percent of slide height and body lines at about 2.7 percent. Node label text is navy #0C1465. Tiles are rectangles with a blue gradient from #0B3C8C at the upper left to #0AA8DC at the lower right, with no rounded corners and no shadow. The outer frame is a 1 px cyan #1EC8F0 outline with no fill. Title is about 6 percent of slide height; subtitle about 3.5 percent. Accent colour is cyan #2BD4F5.

### Slide 54

_Long standard legal text on this slide is summarised, not transcribed; its exact words are in the measured list below._

**Text**

Advanced / Packaging / Industry-first / breakthroughs / in glass substrates / intel innovation

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a left text column starts at about 9% of slide width and holds the title and subtitle stacked and left-aligned, vertically centred between about 30% and 65% of slide height. A large illustrated wafer fills the right half from about 41% to 95% of width. The Intel Innovation logo sits in the bottom-left corner at about 8% from the left edge and 4% from the bottom. There is no visible footer, slide number or margin rule. No legal text is present, so nothing is summarised.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed background image covers the slide. The right-hand element is an illustration of a round silicon-style wafer, shown at a tilted, perspective angle as a dark blue ellipse with a grid of roughly 110 small rectangular gold-copper chiplets, arranged in diagonal rows. The chiplets have a warm peach-gold metallic finish with subtle shading and sit on a textured blue surface. Behind the wafer at upper right are soft light streaks and a blurred lens-flare of pale green, cyan and orange light. On the left edge is a faint, blurred circuit-board and chip-package photograph in dark blue and grey tones. The lighting is a cool blue ambient glow with warm highlights on the chiplets. The composition is full-bleed, with no crop, no box and no border. A dark blue overlay tints the whole image toward the left.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue gradient running from bright cobalt #0A5FD6 at the top left to dark navy #071A5C at the bottom right. Title text is a bright sky-blue to cyan gradient, approximately #1FA6F0 on the left shifting to #5FE3F5 on the right. Subtitle text is a pale warm beige, approximately #E6D7B2. The logo is white (#FFFFFF) with the 'on' in cyan #29C5F6. The typeface for the title is a geometric sans-serif similar to Intel Clear, in a medium or semi-bold weight, with tight letter spacing. The subtitle is a light-weight geometric sans-serif similar to Intel Clear Light. Title text height is about 9.5% of slide height per line. Subtitle text height is about 4.8% of slide height per line, with line spacing about 6%. Shapes have no outlines. There are no corner radii, no shadows and no separate gradients beyond the text fills and the background.

### Slide 55

**Text**

$100 Billion
U.S. Investments
Oregon
Arizona
New Mexico
Ohio
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark navy slide with a left-aligned title block in the upper-left third, a horizontal row of four equal photos across the middle (about 45.5% to 71% of slide height), and their captions centred beneath each photo. The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left and 90% from the top. The title is not in a top title bar, and there is no footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background photograph: a glowing satellite-style night view of the contiguous United States, drawn as cyan-blue state boundary lines and city lights (warm orange-white points) on deep navy, with the coastline and Great Lakes visible on the upper right. Four rectangular, sharp-cornered construction and facility photographs in a row, each with a thin light border. (1) Oregon: aerial of a large construction site with yellow and blue tower cranes, concrete foundation slabs and grey walls, daytime, mostly white and grey with some green edge vegetation. (2) Arizona: aerial of a construction site in a flat brown desert city, a tall yellow crane, a white partly built structure, distant mountains under a hazy pale sky. (3) New Mexico: aerial of a beige multi-storey office-style building with a dark solar-panel roof, a landscaped parking lot with parked cars and trees, brown mountains behind and a blue sky with cumulus clouds. (4) Ohio: aerial of a large earthwork site with brown dirt, grey and white long roofed structures, excavators and trucks, and a lot of bare ground. All four photos are full, uncropped rectangles at the same height, with no overlay tint. A vertical colour-gradient strip (orange, yellow, magenta and violet fading into navy) runs down the left edge, about 0 to 4% of slide width. The Intel innovation logo is a white 'intel' wordmark with a small trademark mark, followed by 'innovation' in white with the final 'on' in cyan.

**Design**

Background is deep navy blue with a violet-to-navy tint behind the left strip and the photograph, with no border. The title '$100 Billion' is in a bold geometric sans-serif (similar to Intel Clear Bold) with a horizontal gradient from light cyan #7BE3F7 on the left to medium blue #2FB8E8 on the right. The subtitle 'U.S. Investments' is in a light-weight geometric sans-serif in cyan #3FD0F5 to #2AAEE0, with normal letter spacing. Captions are medium-weight geometric sans-serif in cyan #3CC8F2 with a slight gradient. Title cap height is about 5.5% of slide height; the subtitle is about 3.5% and the captions about 3.3%. Photo frames are thin light-grey-white lines of about 1 px with square corners and no shadow. No shape lines or accent bars, apart from the left gradient strip.

A single cyan-outlined frame spans most of the width, containing two stacked panels: a board-member logo grid on top and a five-column contributor name list below, with the Intel Innovation logo bottom left and the footer line centred-left near the bottom. Left-aligned text in columns; no slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Logo grid of 12 board-member logos in two rows on a white panel: row 1 Alibaba Group (orange swoosh with wordmark), AMD (black wordmark with arrow), arm (cyan wordmark), ASE GROUP (black wordmark with green-and-orange emblem), Google Cloud (multicolour wordmark), intel (blue wordmark); row 2 Meta (blue infinity symbol with wordmark), Microsoft (four-colour squares with grey wordmark), NVIDIA (green eye emblem with black wordmark), Qualcomm (blue wordmark), SAMSUNG (black wordmark), tsmc (red and black wordmark above a chip-grid emblem with red underline). Contributor section is a five-column bulleted text list with about 75 company names; no chart, table or photograph. Background is a blurred photograph of a data-centre corridor with a dark navy tint, with orange and purple light bleeding in on the left edge.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy #0B0F4F gradient with blurred data-centre photograph and violet and orange light on the left edge. Dark navy background with a blue-to-violet gradient and blurred data-centre photographic texture on the left edge. Large '120+' in cyan gradient #3FD3F5 to #1EA7FF; 'Members… and growing!' in light cyan #5FD4F5, thin geometric sans-serif (Intel One Display Light style). Panel header bars in semi-transparent blue gradient #1E5FA0 with light cyan #5FD4F5 bold sans-serif titles (Intel One Display Medium), about 2.5% of slide height. Frame is a 0.3% cyan #3FC8F0 outline. Panels are white #FFFFFF with no rounded corners and no shadows. Body list text in dark navy #001E50, Intel One Display Regular, about 1.46% of slide height; bullets are navy round dots. Footer text white #FFFFFF, Intel One Display Light, 1.25% of slide height.

### Slide 57

**Text**

Advanced packaging / technology codenamed / Pike Creek / Industry Interoperability Demo / Synopsys UCIe / TSMC N3E Technology / Intel UCIe / Intel 3 Technology / EMIB / Advanced Packaging Technology / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark photographic background with a large translucent blue panel on the left-centre holding the package diagram. The title block is right-aligned to a left edge at about 58.5% width, stacked vertically in the upper-middle, with the three partner logos in a row beneath it. The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 8% from the left and 92% from the top. There is no visible slide number or footer.

**Visuals**

Diagram and photograph. A translucent blue rectangle (x 6.6%, y 28.3%, w 47.2%, h 58.5%) with a thin light-blue outline contains the package schematic. At the top centre, a rectangular photograph (x 22.5%, y 23.4%, w 15.3%, h 20.2%) overlaps the top edge of the panel; it shows a pale grey-green circuit board with white pad rows along its left and right edges, a small green-and-purple iridescent die near the top, and a large matte black square chip in the centre. Below, two labelled bars: a purple-to-pink gradient bar (x 10.9%, y 57.8%, w 17.1%, h 4.0%) labelled Synopsys UCIe over TSMC N3E Technology, and an olive-green gradient bar (x 31.0%, y 57.8%, w 17.2%, h 4.0%) labelled Intel UCIe over Intel 3 Technology. Beneath them, a lighter blue rectangle (x 7.7%, y 63.1%, w 43.9%, h 16.7%) holds a cluster of horizontal glowing cyan lines (x 18.5%, y 64.4%, w 21.4%, h 4.9%) crossed by two vertical cyan ticks at the left and right ends, with the label EMIB above Advanced Packaging Technology. Partner logos on the right: intel wordmark in white, SYNOPSYS wordmark in white, and the tsmc logo in red on a white wafer circle with a red underline. The Intel Innovation logo is white with the letters on and the oo in cyan.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy #0A0F4A to indigo #15157A photographic gradient with blue-tinted circuit-board imagery across the full slide. Dark navy-to-indigo background with a blue-tinted photo of circuit-board traces and heatsink fins, dimmed by a deep navy overlay. Title Advanced packaging technology codenamed is light cyan #7FD8F5 in a light-weight geometric sans with wide letter-spacing; Pike Creek is bold light cyan-blue #6FD3F7 in a geometric sans roughly 12% of slide height for the cap height region; the subtitle Industry Interoperability Demo is bold cyan #3FC6F0. Body labels are white #FFFFFF in a light geometric sans with wide tracking. Accent colours: cyan #2BC4F2, purple gradient #9A5FB0 to #D7A1E3, olive gradient #7FA83A to #5C7A25, red #E01F26 for the tsmc mark. Panel fill is #0A6FB0 at about 60% opacity; the EMIB block fill is #0A7CC0. Lines are thin pale-blue outlines about 1 px. Shapes have square corners with no visible shadow.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100: Full-bleed background photograph of circuit-board traces and heatsink fins tinted blue, with a navy overlay (Covers the whole slide; darkened by a navy overlay; bright orange-red glow at the far left edge)
- shape at x 6.6, y 28.3, w 47.2, h 58.5 [#0A6FB0]: Translucent blue panel (About 60% opacity; thin light-blue outline about 1 px; square corners)
- shape at x 7.7, y 63.1, w 43.9, h 16.7 [#0A7CC0]: EMIB block (Square corners; slightly lighter than panel; contains EMIB label and cyan line cluster)
- photo at x 22.5, y 23.4, w 15.3, h 20.2: Photograph of package board with black chip and small green die (Boxed rectangle with thin light outline; overlaps top edge of blue panel; pale grey-green board colour)
- shape at x 10.9, y 57.8, w 17.1, h 4 [#9A5FB0]: Purple gradient bar (Gradient from #9A5FB0 left to #D7A1E3 right; square corners)
- shape at x 31, y 57.8, w 17.2, h 4 [#7FA83A]: Olive-green gradient bar (Gradient from #7FA83A to #5C7A25; square corners)

### Slide 58

**Text**

3-Dimensional Silicon
Large Language Model
computation needs the highest
performance silicon
intel innovation

**Layout**

A full-bleed isometric chip illustration fills the left and centre of the slide, with the title block set in the right-hand third, vertically centred slightly above the middle. The title and subtitle are left-aligned on a common left edge at about 51.8% of slide width. The intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 8% from the left and 92% from the top. There is no footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Isometric 3D rendering of semiconductor packages on a dark blue background. Left: a blue substrate package with a yellow-gold interposer holding two blue AI Engines & CPUs dies, a RAS block, Security and Debug blocks, UCIe tabs in purple, Fuses and USB tiles in pale blue, a yellow Noncoherent Fabric block, a PCIe G6 tile, a Security tile, and green 224G and dark GPIOs tiles. Centre: a dark grey-navy die with a gold interposer carrying four blue AI Engines & CPUs dies, a grey RAS block, yellow Security and blue Debug blocks, and purple UCIe tabs along its edges. Right: a small dark grey die with Fuses and USB tiles, a gold Noncoherent Fabric block, Security, PCIe G6 (two tiles), 224G and GPIOs tiles, with a dashed link between PCIe G6 tiles. Stacks of grey HBM3 memory cubes with green bases appear around the dies, four on the top and sides of the left and centre packages and one at the bottom centre and bottom left. Background behind the chips is a blurred, dark data-centre server rack with a glowing grid of small cubes in a diagonal band across the top. Lighting is cool blue with warm gold accents on the interposers. The illustration is full-bleed and not boxed or cropped at the slide edges except the left edge where a warm orange-to-violet glow fades in.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy-indigo gradient with a blue glow, #0A0A5C to #1A2A8C, with a blurred server-rack photograph behind the chips. Dark navy to indigo background with a blue glow. Title in a bright sky-blue to teal gradient, geometric sans-serif (close to Intel One Display or similar) in medium weight, large relative to the slide. Subtitle and body in light cyan #5FD4F5 and pale blue, light weight sans-serif, about 3.5% of slide height. Logo is white with the word 'on' in cyan #00C7FD. Chips use flat, saturated fills with soft shadows and no rounded corners; UCIe tabs are violet #7B2FF7 and HBM stacks are grey-lilac with green #3DDC3D bases.

### Slide 59

**Text**

intel labs
Leading efforts to advance
Neuromorphic Computing
LAVA
Open-source
software framework
Codename Loihi 2
on Intel 4
Codename Kapoho Point
8-chip stackable
intel innovation

**Layout**

Centred composition on a full-bleed dark indigo background. The Intel Labs lockup, two-line tagline, LAVA logo and 'Open-source software framework' caption stack down the horizontal centre line from about 12% to 56% of slide height. Two product photos sit in the lower half: Loihi 2 chip at left (about 15% to 41% wide) and Kapoho Point board at right (about 54% to 85% wide), each with a caption beneath it. The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left edge and 90% from the top. There is no title placeholder, slide number or footer.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration of a glowing neural network mesh: thin translucent lines in pale cyan, blue and violet forming polygonal nodes and branches, with soft bokeh dots in green, orange and magenta concentrated at the left and right edges. Photo 1: Intel Loihi 2 neuromorphic chip, shown at a three-quarter top-down angle on a square light-blue ceramic-look package with gold edge contacts and fine circuit traces; a dark rectangular die with multicoloured reflective pattern sits on top of the package centre. Lit from above, cool light-blue and silver tones. Not cropped, shown whole, no tint. Photo 2: Kapoho Point 8-chip stackable board, shown at a three-quarter angle on a gold-edged dark-green PCB with a black edge connector along the top-left edge, four square teal-grey chip packages with silver lids, black capacitors and a black connector at lower right, and small printed text including 'intel labs' and 'KAPOHO POINT REV 0' on the board. Lit from above, dominant tones gold, teal and black. Not cropped, shown whole. LAVA logo: the word LAVA drawn in a hand-brushed red-orange textured stroke with darker red shading, centred. No tint or overlay on the photos.

**Design**

Background deep indigo/navy #14086B with lighter violet glow toward the edges. Intel labs wordmark: 'intel' in white #FFFFFF bold geometric sans, 'labs' in cyan #00C7FD regular weight, with a small registered mark. Tagline 'Leading efforts to advance' in light-weight sans-serif with wide letter-spacing, pale cyan #7FE3FF gradient to #3FCFFF. 'Neuromorphic Computing' in semibold sans-serif with a cyan-to-teal gradient from #5FE6FF to #1FB8D6, size about 5.5% of slide height. 'Open-source' semibold cyan #36D5FF; 'software framework' light-weight wide-tracked cyan #5FD8FF. Captions 'Codename' and 'on Intel 4' in light-weight wide-tracked cyan #5FD8FF, with 'Loihi 2', 'Kapoho Point' in semibold white-cyan #A6EEFF. Typefaces resemble Intel Clear / Intel One Display, a geometric humanist sans-serif. Body and caption text about 2.5% to 3.5% of slide height. Intel Innovation logo in white with 'on' in cyan. No shapes, lines, corner radii or shadows apart from the soft photo edges.

### Slide 60

**Text**

Quantum
Harnessing the power of quantum
physics to solve complex problems
exponentially faster than large-scale
supercomputers
intel innovation

**Layout**

Left-aligned text block sits in the left half, starting at about 9.4% from the left edge and vertically centred between about 27% and 56% of the slide height: the title 'Quantum' on top, then a four-line subtitle, all flush left on a shared left edge. The chip illustration fills the right half and the intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about 7% from the left and 90% from the top. There is no title placeholder, footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark navy illustration covering the whole slide. Right half: a rectangular quantum processor chip, roughly 36% of slide width, seen almost face-on with a slight tilt, its surface gold-yellow at upper left shifting to magenta and violet at lower right, with fine white-gold meander circuit traces, a grid of small square pads around the border and a diamond-shaped emblem at its centre. The chip is lit by a strong magenta glow from its lower right edge and a blue rim light, and it is not cropped. Behind and around the chip, a halftone dot grid in blue and violet forms a curved wave that sweeps from top right to bottom right. Thin glowing streaks in orange, pink and cyan curve across the lower left and bottom right, with a faint orange arc above the chip. At the far top right and right edge, white-blue wiring and connector structures are partly cropped by the slide edge. Left half: fine horizontal light lines in cyan and blue fade out toward the left edge. No photograph, boxed image or tint overlay; the whole illustration is full-bleed.

**Design**

Background is a deep navy-indigo gradient, darkest at the top (about #0A0458) and shading to midnight blue (about #0B1A5C) at the bottom and near the chip. Title 'Quantum' is set in a geometric sans-serif similar to Intel One Display, regular weight, with a horizontal gradient from bright cyan (about #3CE3F7) at left to teal-blue (about #1BA6DC) at right. Subtitle is set in a light-weight geometric sans-serif, regular weight, with each line coloured by a gradient from cyan (about #4FD8F0) through pale aqua-white (about #BFF3FA) to white (about #FFFFFF); the subtitle's first and last words are cyan and the middle words lighter. Title cap height is about 9% of slide height; subtitle body text is about 4.5% of slide height, with tight leading of about 4.3% between baselines. Logo uses a bold sans for 'intel' in white and a light sans for 'innovation' in white with the final 'on' in cyan (about #22C7F0). Line and shape styles: no rules or boxes; the streaks are soft glowing lines with gradient colour (cyan, magenta, orange). No corner radii; the chip has sharp corners with a soft outer glow and a subtle drop shadow.

### Slide 61

**Text**

Intel quantum technology codenamed / Tunnel Falls / 12 / qubit device / 50x50 / nanometers / ~1 Million / times smaller / 95% Yield Rate / across the wafer / Technology performance varies by use, configuration and other factors. Learn more at www.Intel.com/ProcessInnovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark navy background image with a centred stack of text in the middle band of the slide: a small eyebrow line, a large title, a row of three stats with captions, a thin horizontal rule with tick marks, and a centred yield statistic below. A chip photograph sits at the left of the stat row and a large chip illustration fills the right third. The Intel Innovation logo sits bottom-left and the disclaimer line runs along the bottom, right of the logo; no slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a small square quantum chip (dark grey-black package with a gold-tinted die and fine grid pattern) resting on a pinkish skin-toned surface, shown in a small square box at the left of the stat row, sharply focused, warm lighting. Illustration: a large glowing quantum chip rendered at the right side of the slide, tilted slightly, with a yellow-green to magenta-pink gradient glow on a circuit-like pattern of fine gold and violet lines, surrounded by flowing light streaks in orange, pink and blue sweeping from the left and bottom, and a faint dotted halftone texture in the background. Diagram-like detail: a thin cyan horizontal rule spans beneath the three stats with short vertical tick lines dropping from the centre of each stat caption (under '12 qubit device', '50x50 nanometers' and '~1 Million times smaller'). Logo: Intel Innovation wordmark in white with the Intel 'intel' lowercase and 'innovation' with the final 'on' in cyan.

**Design**

Background deep indigo-navy #0B0A4F to #1A0F6B with a diagonal dark-to-lighter gradient; the eyebrow and stat captions are light cyan #5FD4F5 in a geometric sans-serif with regular weight and wide letter-spacing; the title 'Tunnel Falls' is bold cyan-to-light-blue gradient #4FC3F7 to #7FE0FF in a geometric sans-serif (Intel Clear-like); stat numerals are bold white-to-cyan gradient (#7FE7FF to #20B8E8), and the yield figure is bold cyan #1EC8F0 to #7FE0FF; the disclaimer is white small sans-serif. Title text is about 9 percent of slide height; eyebrow about 4.5 percent; stat numerals about 7 percent; captions about 4 percent; body disclaimer about 1.5 percent. Thin cyan #2AB7E8 rule, 1px, no shadow, no corner radii on the rules; the photo box has square corners and no border.

### Slide 62

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small captions and sub-labels on slides 12, 34, 36, 52 and 62 and raise their contrast against the busy art, and simplify the member list on 56.

**Text**

Intel® Quantum SDK
Full-stack approach to quantum computing
Quantum Application
Quantum Compiler
Quantum Runtime
Quantum Control Simulator
Quantum Dot Simulator
Room / Temperature / Electronics
Cryogenic Control Chip
Spin Qubit Chip
intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark indigo background with a wafer disc on the upper left and a cryostat-style quantum hardware tower on the right. Title block is left-aligned at about x 19% starting near y 30%, with a vertical stack of five full-width-of-column layer bars from x 19.2% to 55.3% between y 48% and 88%, each bar's label centred. A column of right-hand row labels starts at x 56.9%, and the Intel innovation logo sits bottom-left at about x 7% y 90%. No page number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Background photograph of a silicon wafer (round, blue-green and magenta sheen, grid of dies) occupying the upper-left circle, partly behind the title, and a close-up render of a dilution refrigerator quantum computer on the right edge, lit with magenta and violet highlights, cropped at the right edge of the slide. Background also contains faint glowing cyan and magenta fibre-like light streaks sweeping from lower-left to right. Five horizontal stacked rectangular bars: Quantum Application (blue gradient, dark blue at left to bright cyan at right), Quantum Compiler (blue gradient), Quantum Runtime (blue gradient), Quantum Control Simulator (violet to lavender gradient), Quantum Dot Simulator (coral red to pink gradient). Right-side labels group the bars: 'Room Temperature Electronics' aligned with the top three bars in cyan-blue, 'Cryogenic Control Chip' in lavender aligned with the fourth bar, 'Spin Qubit Chip' in coral-pink aligned with the fifth bar. Intel innovation wordmark logo in white with the 'on' in cyan.

**Design**

Background deep indigo #1A0A5C fading to dark navy #0B0838 at the edges. Title 'Intel® Quantum SDK' in bold geometric sans-serif (Intel One Display style), cyan-to-light-blue gradient text, #3FD0F5 to #7FB8F0, approximately 8% of slide height cap height. Subtitle in light sans-serif, letter-spaced, cyan #4FD3F0 to pale blue, about 4% of slide height. Layer bars are flat rectangles with gradients, no rounded corners, no shadows, white light-weight sans-serif labels. Right labels are bold sans-serif in cyan #3FD0F5, lavender #C9A0F0 and coral #FF8A8A. Logo white #FFFFFF with cyan accent #2BD9F0.

### Slide 63

**Text**

intel innovation / Lifetime Achievement Award / Dr. Fei-Fei Li / Inaugural Sequoia Professor in the Computer / Science Department at Stanford University, and / Co-Director of Stanford’s Human-Centered AI

**Layout**

Left-aligned text column on the left half, vertically centred in the middle band of the slide, with the logo above the title, the title above a blue panel, and the panel holding the name and credentials. The isometric city illustration fills the lower right, circuit artwork runs across the top right and bottom left, and there is no footer, page number or slide number. Left margin is about 7% of the slide width.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed deep blue digital illustration. Lower right: isometric city of glowing blue, violet and magenta skyscrapers and cube-topped towers standing on a large circular purple-pink wafer with thin white grid lines, rising from the bottom edge and cropped by the right and bottom edges. Upper right: circuit-board artwork in cyan and blue lines with a cloud icon inside a hexagonal outline, a pink code-like bar stack, a glowing red chip, and a dotted pixel grid. Bottom left: a small neon circuit with a cloud outline, a red and yellow chevron chip, a green dot, a blue chevron arrow and a pink-orange code bar stack. Logo and icons: a white outline lightbulb with cyan rays at left of the logo, and the Intel Innovation wordmark in white with the final 'on' in cyan. No photographs.

**Design**

Background is a vertical deep blue gradient, #0A4FB0 at the top fading to #00104A at the bottom-left, with dark navy #000C3A near the centre. Title 'Lifetime Achievement Award' is set in a medium-weight geometric sans with a horizontal gradient from light cyan #5FDDF7 on the left to teal #1AAED6 on the right, about 5.5% of slide height for cap height, sentence case. Name 'Dr. Fei-Fei Li' is semibold sans in pale cyan #BDF0FF, about 4.6% of slide height. Credential lines are light weight sans in white #FFFFFF, about 2.9% of slide height, with line spacing about 2.8% of slide height. Panel is a flat rectangle with a left-to-right gradient from #0B4C8C at the left to #0096D0 at the right, sharp corners, no border, no shadow. Accents are cyan #00C8FF and neon blue #1E90FF in the illustrations.

### Slide 64

**Text**

Bringing AI Everywhere / explore more on newsroom.intel.com / Exceptionally Engineered / AI Platforms / Open & Hardware Agnostic / Software Approach / Confidently Trusted / Developing for the age of / AI PC / December 14, 2023 / Launch / of Intel® / Core™ Ultra / Launch / of 5th Gen / Intel® Xeon® / Advanced / Packaging / Industry-first / breakthroughs / in glass substrates / Software Initiative / codenamed / Project Strata / Quantum / Innovating for the future / UCIe / Universal Chiplet / Interconnect Express / 3-Dimensional / Silicon / intel labs / Neuromorphic / Computing / Beyond 5 Nodes in 4 Years / Looking into the future today / intel / Developer Cloud / Sign up today at cloud.intel.com / OpenVINO / oneAPI / Intel 7 / Intel 4 / Intel 3 / Intel 20A / Intel 18A / Next / Next + / LAVA / intel innovation

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue background with a thin red-pink rectangular frame inset about 8% from the left and right and about 8% from the top and bottom. The title Bringing AI Everywhere sits top-left at x 12.7 y 6.0 on one line, followed by the cyan line explore more on newsroom.intel.com. Content is a mosaic of rectangular panels in three columns: left column x 9.5 to 33.6 (AI platforms list above, Developer Cloud panel below), middle column x 34.1 to 53.5 (December 14 panel above, wide Beyond 5 Nodes panel spanning x 34.2 to 74.2 at the bottom), right column x 74.5 to 93.1 with three stacked panels (Quantum, 3-Dimensional Silicon, Neuromorphic Computing), with the Advanced Packaging and Project Strata panels stacked at x 53.8 to 74.1 between them. Panel gutters are about 0.5% of slide width. The intel innovation wordmark sits bottom-right at about x 75 y 90 inside the frame. No slide number or footer date is shown.

**Visuals**

Background: full-bleed photographic-style digital illustration of a dark blue and purple city/circuit scene. Left edge shows isometric cyan-lit skyscraper blocks and a stacked isometric cube cluster in blue and violet at bottom-left, with white hatched stripes below. Top-right corner shows glowing cyan and white microchip grids and circuit lines. Bottom centre shows short red and cyan horizontal bars resembling a data chart and a small green dotted line with a chevron. Icon: square tile at x 1.5 y 17.0 w 8.6 h 15.2 with a red-to-pink gradient, containing a white outlined hexagon chip and a cyan-and-white stylised brain/AI head graphic. Panel images: Advanced Packaging panel has a wafer photo at x 64.3 y 9.8 w 9.8 h 18.5 (silver circular wafer with rows of small gold-grey dies, cropped by the panel edge). Quantum panel has a square quantum chip render at x 83.0 y 13.2 w 9.3 h 14.4 (gold-yellow and magenta-violet interlocking circuit pattern, centred, dark background). Project Strata panel has an isometric rendering at x 64.9 y 34.2 w 9.1 h 22.7 of stacked blue and purple translucent layers with small screens, cropped at right edge. UCIe panel has an isometric render at x 80.8 y 37.5 w 12.3 h 20.4 of multicolour stacked chiplet blocks in blue, teal, yellow and red. Neuromorphic panel has a render at x 75.1 y 68.0 w 8.1 h 9.4 of a grey-gold chip package with a dark central die and metal heat fins in blue and grey, cropped at the panel edge. Intel Core Ultra box art at x 35.0 y 21.8 w 6.5 h 12.9 (dark navy box with intel CORE ULTRA logo in white and cyan dot grid). Intel Xeon box art at x 35.0 y 36.9 w 6.0 h 12.0 (navy box with intel XEON wordmark in white). Developer Cloud panel has a cloud outline at x 10.0 y 64.4 w 23.1 h 19.8 drawn as a thick rounded stroke with a gradient from magenta through violet to cyan, filled with the panel blue. Inside the cloud: OpenVINO wordmark at x 15.9 y 71.8 w 6.9 h 2.4, oneAPI logo with a large 1 above at x 23.5 y 67.6 w 4.1 h 6.6, and five chip package thumbnails in a row at y 76 to 82.2: Intel Xeon at x 13.2, Intel Xeon Max Series at x 16.4, Intel Data Center GPU at x 19.9, Intel Data Center GPU Max Series at x 23.4, and Intel Gaudi at x 27.2 (each about 3 to 3.2 wide, dark navy with white intel logo). Timeline in Beyond 5 Nodes panel: five process tiles and two Next tiles, each a rounded-corner square with white-to-light grey gradient (first, second, third, fourth and fifth tiles) and white label text, with the Next tiles in pale cyan-blue; tiles at x 36.4, 41.7, 46.8, 52.0, 57.1 (white, width about 4.5, y 72.0 to 80.0) and Next at x 62.8 and Next + at x 68.3 (cyan-blue gradient, width about 4.5). Below each tile a small square tick mark at y 82.5 to 84; a thin white horizontal arrow line runs from x 36.4 to x 73.5 at y 83.6 ending in a triangular arrowhead at the right. Red-outlined border on the Intel 4 tile. Logos: intel wordmark with registered mark at x 10.3 y 47.9 w 21.3 h 12.7 in white on Developer Cloud panel; LAVA wordmark at x 83.0 y 69.0 w 7.5 h 2.5 in red stroked text on the Neuromorphic panel; intel labs wordmark at x 75.7 y 79.5 w 14 h 4.5 with intel in white and labs in cyan; intel innovation wordmark at x 74.0 y 88.1 w 20.0 h 4.5 with intel in white and innovation in white with cyan on the final letters.

**Design**

Background: deep royal blue gradient running from #0B2E9A at upper left to #0A1E7A at lower right, with a violet and magenta glow at far right bottom corner (#7A1FA0). Panels: mid blue #0A7AD0 with cyan #00C7FD overlay at 32 percent opacity, so the panels read as bright sky blue #1A8FE0. Gutter lines are the darker navy #0B2C8F. Title colour: white #FFFFFF. Subtitle explore more on: pale cyan #7FE3FF (light weight), and newsroom.intel.com in bold white. Body text: white #FFFFFF throughout. Accent colours: cyan #00C7FD for the Developer Cloud subheading sign up today at cloud.intel.com and the labs word, and #0C1465 navy for the timeline node labels. Typeface: IntelOne Display, a geometric grotesque sans-serif, in Medium for headlines and Light for body; title size about 4.2 percent of slide height, panel headlines about 3.3 percent, body and sub-lines about 2.5 percent, small timeline labels about 1.2 to 1.7 percent. Shapes: rectangles with square corners on the panels, timeline tiles with small rounded corners about 0.3 percent radius and a soft drop shadow; red frame line #E8565B at about 0.25 percent of slide width. No gradients other than background, tile fills and cloud stroke.

### Slide 65

**Text**

Notices and Disclaimers / For notices, disclaimers, and details / about performance claims, visit / www.intel.com/PerformanceIndex / or scan the QR code: / It / Starts / Here

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue background with decorative horizontal bars at top left and bottom. A block of content sits left of centre: a square QR code at x 20.8% and the heading and text block to its right starting at x 41.2%, vertically centred near y 37% to 60%. A red tag with white text sits at bottom right (x 82.8%, y 70.2%), and the intel innovation logo sits bottom left at x 7.1%, y 90.2%. There is no slide title placeholder, footer, or slide number.
